```
1	import { App, Button, Card, Flex, Form, Input, Select, Typography } from 'antd';
2	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
4	
5	import {
6	  CONNECTION_ENVS,
7	  CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS,
8	  CONNECTION_KINDS,
9	  CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS,
10	  type ConnectionCreate,
11	  type ConnectionKind,
12	  type ConnectionType,
13	  createConnection,
14	  envLabel,
15	  typesOfKind,
16	} from '../api/connections';
17	import { ConnectionTypeFields } from '../components/connections/ConnectionTypeFields';
18	import { initialConfigForType } from '../components/connections/connectionFormSpec';
19	
20	interface FormValues {
21	  name: string;
22	  env: ConnectionCreate['env'];
23	  config?: Record<string, unknown>;
24	  secret?: string;
25	}
26	
27	/**
28	 * Dedicated full-page add-connection flow (GX-Cloud style): pick a type from the
29	 * datasource / orchestration sections, then fill the type-specific form. Editing
30	 * an existing connection still uses the lighter drawer on the Connections page.
31	 */
32	export function ConnectionNew() {
33	  const navigate = useNavigate();
34	  const { message } = App.useApp();
35	  const [type, setType] = useState<ConnectionType>();
36	  const [form] = Form.useForm<FormValues>();
37	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
38	
39	  // Seed the new type's config defaults (e.g. the auth-type) and clear any
40	  // fields left over from a previously-picked type. Runs in an effect — *after*
41	  // the <Form> mounts (it only renders once `type` is truthy) — so the form
42	  // store is connected when we write to it, and so re-picking a type can't leak
43	  // the prior type's name/env (the single useForm store persists across the
44	  // picker round-trip; resetFields wipes it clean).
45	  useEffect(() => {
46	    if (!type) return;
47	    form.resetFields();
48	    form.setFieldsValue({ config: initialConfigForType(type) });
49	  }, [type, form]);
50	
51	  const onFinish = async (values: FormValues) => {
52	    if (!type) return;
53	    setSubmitting(true);
54	    try {
55	      await createConnection({
56	        name: values.name,
57	        type,
58	        env: values.env,
59	        config: values.config ?? {},
60	        secret: values.secret || undefined,
61	      });
62	      message.success(`Connection “${values.name}” created`);
63	      navigate('/connections');
64	    } catch (err) {
65	      message.error(`Create failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
66	    } finally {
67	      setSubmitting(false);
68	    }
69	  };
70	
71	  return (
72	    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>
73	      <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={12}>
74	        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
75	          {type ? `New ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]} connection` : 'New connection'}
76	        </Typography.Title>
77	        <Button onClick={() => (type ? setType(undefined) : navigate('/connections'))}>
78	          {type ? 'Back' : 'Cancel'}
79	        </Button>
80	      </Flex>
81	
82	      {type ? (
83	        <Form form={form} layout="vertical" onFinish={onFinish} requiredMark="optional">
84	          <Form.Item name="name" label="Name" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
85	            <Input />
86	          </Form.Item>
87	          <Form.Item name="env" label="Environment" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
88	            <Select
89	              options={CONNECTION_ENVS.map((e) => ({ value: e, label: envLabel(e) }))}
90	              placeholder="Select an environment"
91	            />
92	          </Form.Item>
93	          <ConnectionTypeFields type={type} form={form} />
94	          <Flex justify="end" gap={8}>
95	            <Button onClick={() => setType(undefined)}>Back</Button>
96	            <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={submitting}>
97	              Create
98	            </Button>
99	          </Flex>
100	        </Form>
101	      ) : (
102	        <Flex vertical gap={24}>
103	          {CONNECTION_KINDS.map((kind) => (
104	            <TypeSection key={kind} kind={kind} types={typesOfKind(kind)} onPick={setType} />
105	          ))}
106	        </Flex>
107	      )}
108	    </Flex>
109	  );
110	}
111	
112	function TypeSection({
113	  kind,
114	  types,
115	  onPick,
116	}: {
117	  kind: ConnectionKind;
118	  types: ConnectionType[];
119	  onPick: (type: ConnectionType) => void;
120	}) {
121	  return (
122	    <Flex vertical gap={12}>
123	      <Typography.Title level={5} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
124	        {CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS[kind]}
125	      </Typography.Title>
126	      <Flex wrap gap={12}>
127	        {types.map((type) => (
128	          <Card
129	            key={type}
130	            hoverable
131	            size="small"
132	            style={{ minWidth: 200 }}
133	            onClick={() => onPick(type)}
134	          >
135	            <Typography.Text strong>{CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]}</Typography.Text>
136	          </Card>
137	        ))}
138	      </Flex>
139	    </Flex>
140	  );
141	}
```

```
1	import { App, Button, Card, Flex, Form, Input, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';
2	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
4	
5	import { getConnection } from '../api/connections';
6	import { createCheck, getSuite } from '../api/suites';
7	import { buildCheckPayload } from '../components/checks/checkForm';
8	import { ConfigFieldItem, SeverityThresholdFields } from '../components/checks/checkFormFields';
9	import { ColumnProfilePanel } from '../components/checks/ColumnProfilePanel';
10	import { DryRunPreview } from '../components/checks/DryRunPreview';
11	import {
12	  EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE,
13	  expectationsByCategoryFor,
14	  type ExpectationCategory,
15	} from '../components/checks/expectationCatalog';
16	import { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';
17	
18	// Monitor-kind categories reserved by ADR 0012 — surfaced (disabled) so the
19	// roadmap is visible; v1 authors GX expectations only.
20	const RESERVED_CATEGORIES = ['Freshness', 'Volume', 'Schema drift'] as const;
21	
22	/**
23	 * Dedicated full-page check authoring flow (GX-Cloud style): pick a category →
24	 * pick an expectation → fill its config + thresholds. Editing an existing check
25	 * still uses the lighter drawer on the suite detail panel.
26	 */
27	export function CheckNew() {
28	  const navigate = useNavigate();
29	  const { suiteId } = useParams<{ suiteId: string }>();
30	  const { message } = App.useApp();
31	  const [category, setCategory] = useState<ExpectationCategory>();
32	  const [expectationType, setExpectationType] = useState<string>();
33	  const [form] = Form.useForm();
34	  const column = Form.useWatch(['config', 'column'], form) as string | undefined;
35	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
36	  // Load the suite + its connection together: the run target (#215) drives the
37	  // dry-run preview's table/schema, and the connection type gates the Custom-SQL
38	  // category (ADR 0019 — SQL datasources only).
39	  const { state } = useAsyncData(async () => {
40	    if (!suiteId) throw new Error('no suite');
41	    const suite = await getSuite(suiteId);
42	    // Best-effort: a suite may be readable while its connection isn't (shared
43	    // suite). The connection only gates the Custom-SQL category — never let its
44	    // absence break the rest of the page (target / dry-run / profiler).
45	    const connection = await getConnection(suite.connection_id).catch(() => null);
46	    return { suite, connection };
47	  });
48	  const target = state.status === 'ok' ? state.data.suite.target : null;
49	  const connectionType = state.status === 'ok' ? state.data.connection?.type : undefined;
50	  const categories = expectationsByCategoryFor(connectionType);
51	
52	  const backToSuite = () => navigate(suiteId ? `/suites/${suiteId}` : '/suites');
53	  const spec = expectationType ? EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE[expectationType] : undefined;
54	
55	  // Start the config form clean each time an expectation is (re)picked — after
56	  // the <Form> mounts (it only renders in the config step), so the store is
57	  // connected and a re-pick can't leak the prior expectation's fields.
58	  useEffect(() => {
59	    if (expectationType) form.resetFields();
60	  }, [expectationType, form]);
61	
62	  const onFinish = async (values: Record<string, unknown>) => {
63	    if (!suiteId || !expectationType) return;
64	    setSubmitting(true);
65	    try {
66	      await createCheck(
67	        suiteId,
68	        buildCheckPayload({ ...values, expectation_type: expectationType }),
69	      );
70	      message.success(`${values.name as string}: created`);
71	      backToSuite();
72	    } catch (err) {
73	      message.error(`Create failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
74	    } finally {
75	      setSubmitting(false);
76	    }
77	  };
78	
79	  // Step 3 — config + thresholds for the chosen expectation.
80	  if (spec) {
81	    return (
82	      <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>
83	        <Header
84	          title={spec.label}
85	          onBack={() => setExpectationType(undefined)}
86	          backLabel="Back to expectations"
87	        />
88	        <Form form={form} layout="vertical" onFinish={onFinish}>
89	          <Typography.Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ marginTop: -8 }}>
90	            {spec.description}
91	          </Typography.Paragraph>
92	          <Form.Item name="name" label="Name" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
93	            <Input placeholder="e.g. order_id not null" />
94	          </Form.Item>
95	          {spec.fields.map((field) => (
96	            <ConfigFieldItem key={field.name} field={field} />
97	          ))}
98	          <SeverityThresholdFields />
99	          {suiteId && (
100	            <>
101	              <Form.Item>
102	                <ColumnProfilePanel suiteId={suiteId} target={target} column={column} />
103	              </Form.Item>
104	              <Form.Item>
105	                <DryRunPreview
106	                  suiteId={suiteId}
107	                  expectationType={expectationType}
108	                  target={target}
109	                  form={form}
110	                />
111	              </Form.Item>
112	            </>
113	          )}
114	          <Flex justify="end" gap={8}>
115	            <Button onClick={() => setExpectationType(undefined)}>Back</Button>
116	            <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={submitting}>
117	              Create check
118	            </Button>
119	          </Flex>
120	        </Form>
121	      </Flex>
122	    );
123	  }
124	
125	  // Step 2 — pick an expectation within the chosen category.
126	  if (category) {
127	    const group = categories.find((g) => g.category === category);
128	    return (
129	      <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 720 }}>
130	        <Header
131	          title={category}
132	          onBack={() => setCategory(undefined)}
133	          backLabel="Back to categories"
134	        />
135	        <Flex wrap gap={12}>
136	          {group?.specs.map((e) => (
137	            <Card
138	              key={e.type}
139	              hoverable
140	              size="small"
141	              style={{ width: 320 }}
142	              onClick={() => setExpectationType(e.type)}
143	            >
144	              <Typography.Text strong>{e.label}</Typography.Text>
145	              <Typography.Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 12 }}>
146	                {e.description}
147	              </Typography.Paragraph>
148	            </Card>
149	          ))}
150	        </Flex>
151	      </Flex>
152	    );
153	  }
154	
155	  // Step 1 — pick a category.
156	  return (
157	    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 720 }}>
158	      <Header title="New check" onBack={backToSuite} backLabel="Cancel" />
159	      <Flex wrap gap={12}>
160	        {categories.map((g) => (
161	          <Card
162	            key={g.category}
163	            hoverable
164	            size="small"
165	            style={{ width: 220 }}
166	            onClick={() => setCategory(g.category)}
167	          >
168	            <Typography.Text strong>{g.category}</Typography.Text>
169	            <Typography.Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 12 }}>
170	              {g.specs.length} expectation{g.specs.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}
171	            </Typography.Paragraph>
172	          </Card>
173	        ))}
174	        {RESERVED_CATEGORIES.map((label) => (
175	          <Card key={label} size="small" style={{ width: 220, opacity: 0.55 }}>
176	            <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={8}>
177	              <Typography.Text strong>{label}</Typography.Text>
178	              <Tag>v1.x</Tag>
179	            </Flex>
180	            <Typography.Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 12 }}>
181	              Auto-monitor — coming soon
182	            </Typography.Paragraph>
183	          </Card>
184	        ))}
185	      </Flex>
186	    </Flex>
187	  );
188	}
189	
190	function Header({
191	  title,
192	  onBack,
193	  backLabel,
194	}: {
195	  title: string;
196	  onBack: () => void;
197	  backLabel: string;
198	}) {
199	  return (
200	    <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={12}>
201	      <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
202	        {title}
203	      </Typography.Title>
204	      <Button onClick={onBack}>{backLabel}</Button>
205	    </Flex>
206	  );
207	}
208
```

```
1	import { App, Button, Divider, Drawer, Flex, Form, Input, Select, Typography } from 'antd';
2	import { useEffect } from 'react';
3	
4	import {
5	  CONNECTION_KIND,
6	  CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS,
7	  type Connection,
8	  envLabel,
9	} from '../../api/connections';
10	import { createSuite, type Suite, targetString, updateSuite } from '../../api/suites';
11	import {
12	  asFileFormat,
13	  assembleTarget,
14	  type TargetFormValues,
15	  type TargetKind,
16	  targetKind,
17	} from './suiteTarget';
18	
19	interface SuiteFormValues extends TargetFormValues {
20	  name: string;
21	  description?: string;
22	  connection_id: string;
23	}
24	
25	/**
26	 * Create or edit a suite. `suite === undefined` is create mode (connection is
27	 * chosen and then locked); editing exposes name/description + the run target
28	 * (`connection_id` is immutable on the backend — re-pointing orphans child
29	 * checks). The target is datasource-shaped (#215): the fields shown depend on
30	 * the selected connection's type, and the target is optional (a suite may stay
31	 * targetless = not-yet-runnable, which disables the Run button until it's set).
32	 */
33	export function SuiteDrawer({
34	  open,
35	  suite,
36	  connections,
37	  onClose,
38	  onSaved,
39	}: {
40	  open: boolean;
41	  suite?: Suite;
42	  /** Available connections for the create-mode picker. */
43	  connections: Connection[];
44	  onClose: () => void;
45	  onSaved: () => void;
46	}) {
47	  const { message } = App.useApp();
48	  const [form] = Form.useForm<SuiteFormValues>();
49	  const isEdit = suite !== undefined;
50	  // A suite's connection is its datasource — orchestration providers (ADF/
51	  // Airflow) are never queryable, so they can't back a suite (CLAUDE.md §4, #242).
52	  const datasourceConnections = connections.filter((c) => CONNECTION_KIND[c.type] === 'datasource');
53	
54	  // The target fields follow the active connection's datasource type: fixed on
55	  // edit, live-tracked from the picker on create.
56	  const watchedConnId = Form.useWatch('connection_id', form);
57	  const activeConnId = isEdit ? suite.connection_id : watchedConnId;
58	  const activeConn = connections.find((c) => c.id === activeConnId);
59	  const kind = activeConn ? targetKind(activeConn.type) : null;
60	
61	  // Prefill on open/edit; reset to a blank form for create.
62	  useEffect(() => {
63	    if (!open) return;
64	    if (suite) {
65	      form.setFieldsValue({
66	        name: suite.name,
67	        description: suite.description ?? undefined,
68	        connection_id: suite.connection_id,
69	        target_table: targetString(suite.target, 'table'),
70	        target_schema: targetString(suite.target, 'schema'),
71	        target_catalog: targetString(suite.target, 'catalog'),
72	        target_path: targetString(suite.target, 'path'),
73	        target_format: asFileFormat(targetString(suite.target, 'file_format')),
74	      });
75	    } else {
76	      form.resetFields();
77	    }
78	  }, [open, suite, form]);
79	
80	  const onSubmit = async () => {
81	    let values: SuiteFormValues;
82	    try {
83	      values = await form.validateFields();
84	    } catch {
85	      return; // validation errors render inline
86	    }
87	    // Assemble the datasource-shaped target; flag a partially-filled section
88	    // inline rather than letting the backend 422 on save.
89	    const { target, error } = kind ? assembleTarget(kind, values) : { target: null };
90	    if (error) {
91	      form.setFields([{ name: error.field, errors: [error.message] }]);
92	      return;
93	    }
94	    // The backend update treats a null target as "leave unchanged" (it never
95	    // clears a target back to NULL), so clearing the fields on a suite that has
96	    // a target would silently keep the old one. Say so rather than no-op.
97	    const hadTarget = isEdit && !!suite.target && Object.keys(suite.target).length > 0;
98	    if (hadTarget && target === null) {
99	      message.error('A run target can’t be removed once set — edit it to point elsewhere instead.');
100	      return;
101	    }
102	    try {
103	      if (isEdit) {
104	        await updateSuite(suite.id, {
105	          name: values.name,
106	          description: values.description ?? null,
107	          target,
108	        });
109	        message.success(`${values.name}: saved`);
110	      } else {
111	        await createSuite({
112	          name: values.name,
113	          description: values.description ?? null,
114	          connection_id: values.connection_id,
115	          target,
116	        });
117	        message.success(`${values.name}: created`);
118	      }
119	      onSaved();
120	    } catch (err) {
121	      message.error(`Save failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
122	    }
123	  };
124	
125	  return (
126	    <Drawer
127	      title={isEdit ? `Edit “${suite.name}”` : 'New suite'}
128	      open={open}
129	      onClose={onClose}
130	      width={480}
131	      destroyOnHidden
132	      extra={
133	        <Flex gap={8}>
134	          <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
135	          <Button type="primary" onClick={onSubmit}>
136	            {isEdit ? 'Save' : 'Create'}
137	          </Button>
138	        </Flex>
139	      }
140	    >
141	      <Form form={form} layout="vertical">
142	        <Form.Item name="name" label="Name" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
143	          <Input />
144	        </Form.Item>
145	        <Form.Item name="description" label="Description (optional)">
146	          <Input.TextArea rows={3} />
147	        </Form.Item>
148	        <Form.Item
149	          name="connection_id"
150	          label="Connection"
151	          rules={[{ required: true }]}
152	          extra={isEdit ? 'The connection is fixed once a suite is created.' : undefined}
153	        >
154	          <Select
155	            disabled={isEdit}
156	            placeholder="Select a datasource connection"
157	            options={datasourceConnections.map((c) => ({
158	              value: c.id,
159	              label: `${c.name} · ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[c.type]} · ${envLabel(c.env)}`,
160	            }))}
161	          />
162	        </Form.Item>
163	        {kind && <TargetFields kind={kind} />}
164	      </Form>
165	    </Drawer>
166	  );
167	}
168	
169	/**
170	 * The datasource-shaped run-target inputs. Optional as a whole (leave blank for a
171	 * not-yet-runnable suite); when started, the required field for the datasource is
172	 * enforced at submit by `assembleTarget`. Field names match `TargetFormValues`.
173	 */
174	function TargetFields({ kind }: { kind: TargetKind }) {
175	  return (
176	    <>
177	      <Divider style={{ marginTop: 4 }} />
178	      <Flex vertical gap={2} style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
179	        <Typography.Text strong>Run target</Typography.Text>
180	        <Typography.Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
181	          Where this suite’s checks run. Optional — required to run the suite.
182	        </Typography.Text>
183	      </Flex>
184	
185	      {kind === 'flatfile' ? (
186	        <>
187	          <Form.Item name="target_path" label="File path">
188	            <Input placeholder="container/path/to/data.csv" />
189	          </Form.Item>
190	          <Form.Item name="target_format" label="File format">
191	            <Select
192	              allowClear
193	              placeholder="Infer from extension"
194	              options={[
195	                { value: 'csv', label: 'CSV' },
196	                { value: 'parquet', label: 'Parquet' },
197	              ]}
198	            />
199	          </Form.Item>
200	        </>
201	      ) : (
202	        <>
203	          {kind === 'uc' && (
204	            <Form.Item name="target_catalog" label="Catalog">
205	              <Input placeholder="main" />
206	            </Form.Item>
207	          )}
208	          <Form.Item name="target_schema" label="Schema (optional)">
209	            <Input placeholder={kind === 'uc' ? 'default' : 'PUBLIC'} />
210	          </Form.Item>
211	          <Form.Item name="target_table" label="Table">
212	            <Input placeholder={kind === 'uc' ? 'orders' : 'ANALYTICS.ORDERS'} />
213	          </Form.Item>
214	        </>
215	      )}
216	    </>
217	  );
218	}
219
```

```
38	/**
39	 * A suite's identity block — datasource avatar + name + connection/env — shared
40	 * by the browse-grid card and the master list row so the two never drift. The
41	 * name renders as plain text (never an `<h4>`), so only the detail panel owns
42	 * the suite heading. `description` is opt-in (the grid card shows it; the row
43	 * doesn't).
44	 */
45	function SuiteIdentity({
46	  suite,
47	  conn,
48	  size = 40,
49	  selected = false,
50	  showDescription = false,
51	}: {
52	  suite: Suite;
53	  conn: Connection | undefined;
54	  size?: number;
55	  selected?: boolean;
56	  showDescription?: boolean;
57	}) {
58	  return (
59	    <Flex
60	      gap={12}
61	      align={showDescription ? 'flex-start' : 'center'}
62	      style={{ width: '100%', minWidth: 0 }}
63	    >
64	      {conn && <ConnectionTypeAvatar type={conn.type} size={size} />}
65	      <Flex vertical gap={2} style={{ minWidth: 0, flex: 1 }}>
66	        <Typography.Text strong ellipsis style={selected ? { color: BRAND.primary } : undefined}>
67	          {suite.name}
68	        </Typography.Text>
69	        {conn ? (
70	          <Flex gap={6} align="center">
71	            <Typography.Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }} ellipsis>
72	              {conn.name}
73	            </Typography.Text>
74	            <Tag color={ENV_COLORS[conn.env]} style={{ marginInlineEnd: 0 }}>
75	              {envLabel(conn.env)}
76	            </Tag>
77	          </Flex>
78	        ) : (
79	          <Typography.Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
80	            No connection
81	          </Typography.Text>
82	        )}
83	        {showDescription && suite.description && (
84	          <Typography.Paragraph
85	            type="secondary"
86	            ellipsis={{ rows: 2 }}
87	            style={{ fontSize: 12, margin: 0, marginTop: 2 }}
88	          >
89	            {suite.description}
90	          </Typography.Paragraph>
91	        )}
92	      </Flex>
93	    </Flex>
94	  );
95	}
96	
97	export function Suites() {
98	  const navigate = useNavigate();
99	  // The selected suite is the route (`/suites/:suiteId`) so it deep-links and
100	  // survives the round-trip to the check editor; `/suites` selects nothing.
101	  const { suiteId } = useParams<{ suiteId: string }>();
102	  const selectedId = suiteId ?? null;
103	  const { state, reload } = useAsyncData(listSuites);
104	  const { state: connState } = useAsyncData(listConnections);
105	  // `drawer.suite === undefined` while open = create mode; a suite = edit.
106	  const [drawer, setDrawer] = useState<{ open: boolean; suite?: Suite }>({ open: false });
107	  const [importOpen, setImportOpen] = useState(false);
108	
109	  const connections = connState.status === 'ok' ? connState.data : [];
110	  // A suite binds to a datasource only — creating/importing needs at least one
111	  // (orchestration providers don't count; CLAUDE.md §4, #242).
112	  const hasDatasource = connections.some((c) => CONNECTION_KIND[c.type] === 'datasource');
113	
114	  return (
115	    <Flex vertical gap={24}>
116	      <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={12}>
117	        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
118	          Suites
119	        </Typography.Title>
120	        <Flex gap={8}>
121	          <Button
122	            loading={connState.status === 'loading'}
123	            disabled={!hasDatasource}
124	            onClick={() => setImportOpen(true)}
125	          >
126	            Import
127	          </Button>
128	          <Button
129	            type="primary"
130	            loading={connState.status === 'loading'}
131	            disabled={!hasDatasource}
132	            onClick={() => setDrawer({ open: true })}
133	          >
134	            New suite
135	          </Button>
136	        </Flex>
137	      </Flex>
138	      {connState.status === 'error' && (
139	        // Suites can still be viewed/deleted, but creating one needs the
140	        // connection list — surface the failure rather than silently disabling.
141	        <Alert
142	          type="warning"
143	          showIcon
144	          title="Couldn’t load connections"
145	          description={`Creating a suite is unavailable until connections load. ${connState.error}`}
146	        />
147	      )}
148	      <SuitesBody
149	        state={state}
150	        connections={connections}
151	        selectedId={selectedId}
152	        onSelect={(id) => navigate(`/suites/${id}`)}
153	        onEdit={(suite) => setDrawer({ open: true, suite })}
154	        onDeleted={() => {
155	          navigate('/suites');
156	          reload();
157	        }}
158	      />
159	      <SuiteDrawer
160	        open={drawer.open}
161	        suite={drawer.suite}
162	        connections={connections}
163	        onClose={() => setDrawer({ open: false })}
164	        onSaved={() => {
165	          setDrawer({ open: false });
166	          reload();
167	        }}
168	      />
169	      <ImportSuiteDrawer
170	        open={importOpen}
171	        connections={connections}
172	        onClose={() => setImportOpen(false)}
173	        onImported={(suite) => {
174	          setImportOpen(false);
175	          reload();
176	          navigate(`/suites/${suite.id}`);
177	        }}
178	      />
179	    </Flex>
180	  );
181	}
182	
183	function SuitesBody({
184	  state,
185	  connections,
186	  selectedId,
187	  onSelect,
188	  onEdit,
189	  onDeleted,
190	}: {
191	  state: AsyncState<Suite[]>;
192	  connections: Connection[];
193	  selectedId: string | null;
194	  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
195	  onEdit: (suite: Suite) => void;
196	  onDeleted: () => void;
197	}) {
198	  if (state.status === 'loading') {
199	    return <Spin tip="Loading suites…" size="large" style={{ marginTop: 80 }} />;
200	  }
201	  if (state.status === 'error') {
202	    return (
203	      <Alert
204	        type="error"
205	        showIcon
206	        title="Failed to load suites"
207	        description={state.error}
208	        style={{ margin: 24 }}
209	      />
210	    );
211	  }
212	  const suites = state.data;
213	  if (suites.length === 0) {
214	    return <Empty description="No suites yet — create one to start authoring checks." />;
215	  }
216	  const selected = suites.find((s) => s.id === selectedId) ?? null;
217	
218	  // Nothing selected → a full-width grid of suite cards that fills the page,
219	  // rather than a narrow list beside a big empty panel. Picking one switches to
220	  // the focused master-detail (the list stays, for quick switching).
221	  if (!selected) {
222	    return <SuiteGrid suites={suites} connections={connections} onSelect={onSelect} />;
223	  }
224	
225	  return (
226	    <Flex gap={24} align="flex-start">
227	      <Card size="small" style={{ width: 320, flexShrink: 0 }} styles={{ body: { padding: 0 } }}>
228	        <List
229	          dataSource={suites}
230	          renderItem={(suite) => {
231	            const conn = connections.find((c) => c.id === suite.connection_id);
232	            const isSelected = suite.id === selectedId;
233	            return (
234	              <List.Item
235	                onClick={() => onSelect(suite.id)}
236	                className="dq-suite-row"
237	                style={{
238	                  cursor: 'pointer',
239	                  padding: '12px 16px',
240	                  background: isSelected ? BRAND.selectedBg : undefined,
241	                }}
242	              >
243	                <SuiteIdentity suite={suite} conn={conn} size={34} selected={isSelected} />
244	              </List.Item>
245	            );
246	          }}
247	        />
248	      </Card>
249	      <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
250	        <SuiteDetail
251	          key={selected.id}
252	          suite={selected}
253	          connections={connections}
254	          onEdit={() => onEdit(selected)}
255	          onDeleted={onDeleted}
256	        />
257	      </div>
258	    </Flex>
259	  );
260	}
261	
262	/** Browse view: suite cards in a responsive grid that fills the page width. */
263	function SuiteGrid({
264	  suites,
265	  connections,
266	  onSelect,
267	}: {
268	  suites: Suite[];
269	  connections: Connection[];
270	  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
271	}) {
272	  return (
273	    <div
274	      style={{
275	        display: 'grid',
276	        gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))',
277	        gap: 16,
278	      }}
279	    >
280	      {suites.map((suite) => {
281	        const conn = connections.find((c) => c.id === suite.connection_id);
282	        return (
283	          <Card
284	            key={suite.id}
285	            size="small"
286	            className="dq-card--interactive"
287	            style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}
288	            onClick={() => onSelect(suite.id)}
289	          >
290	            <SuiteIdentity suite={suite} conn={conn} showDescription />
291	          </Card>
292	        );
293	      })}
294	    </div>
295	  );
296	}
297	
298	function SuiteDetail({
299	  suite,
300	  connections,
301	  onEdit,
302	  onDeleted,
303	}: {
304	  suite: Suite;
305	  connections: Connection[];
306	  onEdit: () => void;
307	  onDeleted: () => void;
308	}) {
309	  const { message, modal } = App.useApp();
310	  const navigate = useNavigate();
311	  // Remounted (keyed by suite.id) when the selection changes → checks refetch.
312	  const { state, reload } = useAsyncData(() => listChecks(suite.id));
313	  const connection = connections.find((c) => c.id === suite.connection_id);
314	  // The edit drawer (create is the dedicated /checks/new page) → open iff editing.
315	  const [editingCheck, setEditingCheck] = useState<Check | null>(null);
316	
317	  const [exporting, setExporting] = useState(false);
318	  const [shareOpen, setShareOpen] = useState(false);
319	  // The live-progress drawer opens on the run id returned by a manual trigger.
320	  const [progressRunId, setProgressRunId] = useState<string | null>(null);
321	  // Managing shares (and deleting) needs admin; the read stamps the caller's level.
322	  const canManage = canManageSuite(suite);
323	  // Triggering a run is edit-gated (matches the backend); a null target isn't runnable.
324	  const canRun = canRunSuite(suite);
325	
326	  // Open the live-progress drawer on the queued run rather than bouncing to
327	  // /results — the user watches it execute check-by-check (and can cancel).
328	  const { running, run } = useRunTrigger((queued) => setProgressRunId(queued.id));
329	
330	  const onExport = async () => {
331	    setExporting(true);
332	    try {
333	      const doc = await exportSuite(suite.id);
334	      downloadJson(`${toFilenameStem(suite.name)}.json`, doc);
335	    } catch (err) {
336	      message.error(`Export failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
337	    } finally {
338	      setExporting(false);
339	    }
340	  };
341	
342	  const onDelete = () => {
343	    modal.confirm({
344	      title: `Delete “${suite.name}”?`,
345	      content: 'This removes the suite and all of its checks.',
346	      okText: 'Delete',
347	      okType: 'danger',
348	      onOk: async () => {
349	        try {
350	          await deleteSuite(suite.id);
351	          message.success(`${suite.name} deleted`);
352	          onDeleted();
353	        } catch (err) {
354	          message.error(`Delete failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
355	          throw err; // keep the confirm modal open on failure
356	        }
357	      },
358	    });
359	  };
360	
361	  return (
362	    <Flex vertical gap={16}>
363	      <Flex justify="space-between" align="flex-start" gap={12}>
364	        <Flex vertical gap={6}>
365	          <Typography.Title level={4} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
366	            {suite.name}
367	          </Typography.Title>
368	          {connection ? (
369	            <Flex gap={8} align="center">
370	              <Typography.Text type="secondary">
371	                {connection.name} · {CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[connection.type]}
372	              </Typography.Text>
373	              <Tag color={ENV_COLORS[connection.env]}>{envLabel(connection.env)}</Tag>
374	            </Flex>
375	          ) : (
376	            <Typography.Text type="secondary">Connection {suite.connection_id}</Typography.Text>
377	          )}
378	        </Flex>
379	        <Flex gap={8}>
380	          {canRun &&
381	            (suite.target ? (
382	              <Button
383	                type="primary"
384	                icon={<PlayCircleOutlined />}
385	                loading={running}
386	                onClick={() => run(suite)}
387	              >
388	                Run
389	              </Button>
390	            ) : (
391	              // No target yet → not runnable; show why rather than a 422 on click.
392	              // The <span> is required: a disabled antd Button has pointer-events:
393	              // none, so the Tooltip must hover the wrapper, not the button.
394	              <Tooltip title="Set a run target (Edit) before running this suite">
395	                <span style={{ cursor: 'not-allowed' }}>
396	                  <Button type="primary" icon={<PlayCircleOutlined />} disabled>
397	                    Run
398	                  </Button>
399	                </span>
400	              </Tooltip>
401	            ))}
402	          <Button onClick={() => setShareOpen(true)}>Share</Button>
403	          <Button loading={exporting} onClick={onExport}>
404	            Export
405	          </Button>
406	          <Button onClick={onEdit}>Edit</Button>
407	          <Button danger onClick={onDelete}>
408	            Delete
409	          </Button>
410	        </Flex>
411	      </Flex>
412	      {suite.description && <Typography.Paragraph>{suite.description}</Typography.Paragraph>}
413	      <ChecksList
414	        suiteId={suite.id}
415	        state={state}
416	        onAdd={() => navigate(`/suites/${suite.id}/checks/new`)}
417	        onEdit={(check) => setEditingCheck(check)}
418	        onChanged={reload}
419	      />
420	      {/* Triggers + schedules are edit-gated (same as runs): a pipeline/DAG bound
421	          here runs the suite on its success; a schedule runs it on a cron cadence.
422	          canRun is exactly the edit-level capability. */}
423	      <TriggersPanel suiteId={suite.id} canManage={canRun} />
424	      <SchedulesPanel suiteId={suite.id} canManage={canRun} />
425	      <CheckDrawer
426	        open={editingCheck !== null}
427	        suiteId={suite.id}
428	        check={editingCheck ?? undefined}
429	        target={suite.target}
430	        connectionType={connection?.type}
431	        onClose={() => setEditingCheck(null)}
432	        onSaved={() => {
433	          setEditingCheck(null);
434	          reload();
435	        }}
436	      />
437	      <SharePanel
438	        open={shareOpen}
439	        suiteId={suite.id}
440	        ownerId={suite.created_by}
441	        canManage={canManage}
442	        onClose={() => setShareOpen(false)}
443	      />
444	      <LiveRunProgress
445	        runId={progressRunId}
446	        suiteName={suite.name}
447	        canManage={canRun}
448	        onClose={() => setProgressRunId(null)}
449	      />
450	    </Flex>
451	  );
452	}
453	
454	function ChecksList({
455	  suiteId,
456	  state,
457	  onAdd,
458	  onEdit,
459	  onChanged,
460	}: {
461	  suiteId: string;
462	  state: AsyncState<Check[]>;
463	  onAdd: () => void;
464	  onEdit: (check: Check) => void;
465	  onChanged: () => void;
466	}) {
467	  const { message, modal } = App.useApp();
468	
469	  const onDelete = (check: Check) => {
470	    modal.confirm({
471	      title: `Delete “${check.name}”?`,
472	      okText: 'Delete',
473	      okType: 'danger',
474	      onOk: async () => {
475	        try {
476	          await deleteCheck(suiteId, check.id);
477	          message.success(`${check.name} deleted`);
478	          onChanged();
479	        } catch (err) {
480	          message.error(`Delete failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
481	          throw err; // keep the confirm modal open on failure
482	        }
483	      },
484	    });
485	  };
486	
487	  if (state.status === 'loading') {
488	    return <Spin tip="Loading checks…" />;
489	  }
490	  if (state.status === 'error') {
491	    return <Alert type="error" showIcon title="Failed to load checks" description={state.error} />;
492	  }
493	  const checks = state.data;
494	  return (
495	    <Card
496	      size="small"
497	      title={`Checks (${checks.length})`}
498	      extra={
499	        <Button type="primary" size="small" onClick={onAdd}>
500	          Add check
501	        </Button>
502	      }
503	    >
504	      {checks.length === 0 ? (
505	        <Empty
506	          image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE}
507	          description="No checks yet — add one to start."
508	        />
509	      ) : (
510	        <List
511	          dataSource={checks}
512	          renderItem={(check) => (
513	            <List.Item
514	              actions={[
515	                <Button key="edit" type="link" size="small" onClick={() => onEdit(check)}>
516	                  Edit
517	                </Button>,
518	                <Button
519	                  key="delete"
520	                  type="link"
521	                  size="small"
522	                  danger
523	                  onClick={() => onDelete(check)}
524	                >
525	                  Delete
526	                </Button>,
527	              ]}
528	            >
529	              <Flex vertical gap={2}>
530	                <Typography.Text strong>{check.name}</Typography.Text>
531	                <Typography.Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
532	                  {check.expectation_type}
533	                </Typography.Text>
534	              </Flex>
535	            </List.Item>
536	          )}
537	        />
538	      )}
539	    </Card>
540	  );
541	}
```

```
1	import { MoreOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';

3	  Alert,

14	  Typography,
15	} from 'antd';
16	import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
17	import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
18	
19	import {
20	  CONNECTION_KIND,
21	  CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS,
22	  CONNECTION_KINDS,
23	  CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS,
24	  CONNECTION_TYPES,
25	  type Connection,
26	  type ConnectionType,
27	  deleteConnection,
28	  ENV_COLORS,
29	  envLabel,
30	  listConnections,
31	  testConnection,
32	} from '../api/connections';
33	import { ConnectionDrawer } from '../components/connections/ConnectionDrawer';
34	import { ConnectionTypeAvatar } from '../components/connections/connectionVisuals';
35	import { ReauthModal } from '../components/connections/ReauthModal';
36	import { type AsyncState, useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';
37	
38	/** Live connectivity state for a card — the health-page badge. */
39	type HealthState = 'idle' | 'testing' | 'ok' | 'failed';
40	
41	/** Per-card actions, threaded from the page so they can mutate shared state. */
42	interface ConnectionActions {
43	  onEdit: (connection: Connection) => void;
44	  onReauth: (connection: Connection) => void;
45	  onChanged: () => void;
46	  /** Run a connectivity test and reflect the result on the card's health badge. */
47	  onTest: (connection: Connection) => Promise<boolean>;
48	  /** Drop a connection's stale health entry (after delete / edit / re-auth). */
49	  onClearHealth: (id: string) => void;
50	}
51	
52	/** Group connections by type in one pass, preserving canonical type order. */
53	function groupByType(connections: Connection[]): [ConnectionType, Connection[]][] {
54	  const byType = new Map<ConnectionType, Connection[]>();
55	  for (const c of connections) {
56	    const group = byType.get(c.type);
57	    if (group) group.push(c);
58	    else byType.set(c.type, [c]);
59	  }
60	  return CONNECTION_TYPES.filter((type) => byType.has(type)).map((type) => [
61	    type,
62	    byType.get(type) as Connection[],
63	  ]);
64	}
65	
66	export function Connections() {
67	  const { message } = App.useApp();
68	  const navigate = useNavigate();
69	  const { state, reload } = useAsyncData(listConnections);
70	  // The drawer is edit-only (create is the /connections/new page) → it's open iff
71	  // a connection is being edited.
72	  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Connection | null>(null);
73	  const [reauthing, setReauthing] = useState<Connection | null>(null);
74	  // Per-connection live connectivity status (the bulk health view).
75	  const [health, setHealth] = useState<Record<string, HealthState>>({});
76	  const [testingAll, setTestingAll] = useState(false);
77	
78	  const testOne = useCallback(async (connection: Connection): Promise<boolean> => {
79	    setHealth((h) => ({ ...h, [connection.id]: 'testing' }));
80	    try {
81	      const { ok } = await testConnection(connection.id);
82	      setHealth((h) => ({ ...h, [connection.id]: ok ? 'ok' : 'failed' }));
83	      return ok;
84	    } catch {
85	      setHealth((h) => ({ ...h, [connection.id]: 'failed' }));
86	      return false;
87	    }
88	  }, []);
89	
90	  // Drop a stale health result when the connection changes underneath it —
91	  // after a re-auth, an edit, or a delete the prior pass/fail no longer holds,
92	  // so the badge returns to idle until re-tested (and the map can't leak).
93	  const clearHealth = useCallback((id: string) => {
94	    setHealth((h) =>
95	      id in h ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(h).filter(([key]) => key !== id)) : h,
96	    );
97	  }, []);
98	
99	  const connections = state.status === 'ok' ? state.data : [];
100	
101	  const testAll = async () => {
102	    setTestingAll(true);
103	    const results = await Promise.all(connections.map(testOne));
104	    setTestingAll(false);
105	    const failed = results.filter((ok) => !ok).length;
106	    if (failed === 0) message.success(`All ${results.length} connections healthy`);
107	    else message.warning(`${failed} of ${results.length} connections unreachable`);
108	  };
109	
110	  const actions: ConnectionActions = {
111	    onEdit: setEditing,
112	    onReauth: setReauthing,
113	    onChanged: reload,
114	    onTest: testOne,
115	    onClearHealth: clearHealth,
116	  };
117	
118	  return (
119	    <Flex vertical gap={24}>
120	      <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={12}>
121	        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
122	          Connections
123	        </Typography.Title>
124	        <Flex gap={8}>
125	          <Button loading={testingAll} disabled={connections.length === 0} onClick={testAll}>
126	            Test all
127	          </Button>
128	          <Button type="primary" onClick={() => navigate('/connections/new')}>
129	            Add connection
130	          </Button>
131	        </Flex>
132	      </Flex>
133	      <ConnectionsBody state={state} actions={actions} health={health} />
134	      <ConnectionDrawer
135	        open={editing !== null}
136	        connection={editing ?? undefined}
137	        onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
138	        onSaved={() => {
139	          // An edit may have changed the host/credential → invalidate any prior test.
140	          if (editing) clearHealth(editing.id);
141	          setEditing(null);
142	          reload();
143	        }}
144	      />
145	      <ReauthModal
146	        connection={reauthing}
147	        onClose={() => setReauthing(null)}
148	        onDone={() => {
149	          // Credential rotated → the old unreachable verdict no longer holds.
150	          if (reauthing) clearHealth(reauthing.id);
151	          setReauthing(null);
152	          reload();
153	        }}
154	      />
155	    </Flex>
156	  );
157	}
158	
159	function ConnectionsBody({
160	  state,
161	  actions,
162	  health,
163	}: {
164	  state: AsyncState<Connection[]>;
165	  actions: ConnectionActions;
166	  health: Record<string, HealthState>;
167	}) {
168	  if (state.status === 'loading') {
169	    return <Spin tip="Loading connections…" size="large" style={{ marginTop: 80 }} />;
170	  }
171	  if (state.status === 'error') {
172	    return (
173	      <Alert
174	        type="error"
175	        showIcon
176	        title="Failed to load connections"
177	        description={state.error}
178	        style={{ margin: 24 }}
179	      />
180	    );
181	  }
182	  const connections = state.data;
183	  if (connections.length === 0) {
184	    return <Empty description="No connections configured yet" />;
185	  }
186	  // Two top-level sections (Data sources / Orchestration), each grouping by type.
187	  // A subtle divider reinforces the datasource-vs-orchestration split (the
188	  // load-bearing distinction in DataQ) without competing with the headings.
189	  const sections = CONNECTION_KINDS.map((kind) => ({
190	    kind,
191	    ofKind: connections.filter((c) => CONNECTION_KIND[c.type] === kind),
192	  })).filter((s) => s.ofKind.length > 0);
193	
194	  return (
195	    <Flex vertical gap={24}>
196	      {sections.map(({ kind, ofKind }, i) => (
197	        <Flex key={kind} vertical gap={16}>
198	          {i > 0 && <Divider style={{ margin: '0 0 4px' }} />}
199	          <Typography.Title level={4} style={{ margin: 0 }}>
200	            {CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS[kind]}
201	          </Typography.Title>
202	          {groupByType(ofKind).map(([type, group]) => (
203	            <ConnectionTypeSection
204	              key={type}
205	              type={type}
206	              connections={group}
207	              actions={actions}
208	              health={health}
209	            />
210	          ))}
211	        </Flex>
212	      ))}
213	    </Flex>
214	  );
215	}

217	function ConnectionTypeSection({
218	  type,
219	  connections,
220	  actions,
221	  health,
222	}: {
223	  type: ConnectionType;
224	  connections: Connection[];
225	  actions: ConnectionActions;

229	    <Flex vertical gap={12}>
230	      <Typography.Title level={5} style={{ margin: 0 }}>

232	      </Typography.Title>

235	      <div

254	
255	/** Health badge per connectivity state (the bulk health view's signal). */
256	function HealthBadge({ health }: { health: HealthState }) {
257	  switch (health) {
258	    case 'testing':
259	      return <Badge status="processing" text="testing…" />;
260	    case 'ok':
261	      return <Badge status="success" text="healthy" />;
262	    case 'failed':
263	      return <Badge status="error" text="unreachable" />;
264	    case 'idle':
265	      return null;
266	  }
267	}
268	
269	function ConnectionCard({
270	  connection,
271	  actions,
272	  health,
273	}: {
274	  connection: Connection;
275	  actions: ConnectionActions;
276	  health: HealthState;
277	}) {
278	  const { message, modal } = App.useApp();
279	
280	  const onDelete = () => {
281	    modal.confirm({
282	      title: `Delete “${connection.name}”?`,
283	      content: 'This removes the connection and its stored credential.',
284	      okText: 'Delete',
285	      okType: 'danger',
286	      onOk: async () => {
287	        try {
288	          await deleteConnection(connection.id);
289	          message.success(`${connection.name} deleted`);
290	          actions.onClearHealth(connection.id);
291	          actions.onChanged();
292	        } catch (err) {
293	          message.error(`Delete failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
294	          throw err; // keep the confirm modal open on failure
295	        }
296	      },
297	    });
298	  };
299	
300	  const menuItems = [
301	    { key: 'edit', label: 'Edit', onClick: () => actions.onEdit(connection) },
302	    { key: 'reauth', label: 'Re-authenticate', onClick: () => actions.onReauth(connection) },
303	    { type: 'divider' as const },
304	    { key: 'delete', label: 'Delete', danger: true, onClick: onDelete },
305	  ];
306	
307	  return (
308	    <Card size="small" className="dq-card--interactive">
309	      <Flex justify="space-between" align="center" gap={12}>
310	        <Flex gap={12} align="center" style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
311	          <ConnectionTypeAvatar type={connection.type} />
312	          <Flex vertical gap={6} style={{ minWidth: 0 }}>
313	            <Typography.Text strong ellipsis>
314	              {connection.name}
315	            </Typography.Text>
316	            <Flex gap={8} align="center" wrap>
317	              <Tag color={ENV_COLORS[connection.env]}>{envLabel(connection.env)}</Tag>
318	              {connection.has_secret ? (
319	                <Badge status="success" text="credential set" />
320	              ) : (
321	                <Badge status="warning" text="no credential" />
322	              )}
323	              <HealthBadge health={health} />
324	            </Flex>
325	            {health === 'failed' && (
326	              <Button
327	                type="link"
328	                size="small"
329	                style={{ padding: 0, height: 'auto' }}
330	                onClick={() => actions.onReauth(connection)}
331	              >
332	                Re-authenticate
333	              </Button>
334	            )}
335	          </Flex>
336	        </Flex>
337	        <Flex gap={8} align="center">
338	          <Button
339	            size="small"
340	            loading={health === 'testing'}
341	            onClick={() => actions.onTest(connection)}
342	          >
343	            Test
344	          </Button>
345	          <Dropdown menu={{ items: menuItems }} trigger={['click']}>
346	            <Button
347	              size="small"
348	              icon={<MoreOutlined />}
349	              aria-label={`${connection.name} actions`}
350	            />
351	          </Dropdown>
352	        </Flex>
353	      </Flex>
354	    </Card>
355	  );
356	}
357
```

```
1	import { App, Button, Drawer, Flex, Form, Input, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';
2	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	
4	import {
5	  CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS,
6	  type Connection,
7	  ENV_COLORS,
8	  envLabel,
9	  updateConnection,
10	} from '../../api/connections';
11	import { ConnectionTypeFields } from './ConnectionTypeFields';
12	
13	interface FormValues {
14	  name: string;
15	  config?: Record<string, unknown>;
16	}
17	
18	/**
19	 * Edit an existing connection. Type + env are immutable (the backend
20	 * `ConnectionUpdate` rejects them) and shown read-only; the secret is omitted —
21	 * credential rotation is the separate Re-auth flow. Creating a connection is a
22	 * dedicated page (`/connections/new`), not this drawer.
23	 */
24	export function ConnectionDrawer({
25	  open,
26	  onClose,
27	  onSaved,
28	  connection,
29	}: {
30	  open: boolean;
31	  onClose: () => void;
32	  /** Called after a successful update (so the list can refresh). */
33	  onSaved: () => void;
34	  connection?: Connection;
35	}) {
36	  const { message } = App.useApp();
37	  const [form] = Form.useForm<FormValues>();
38	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
39	
40	  // Prefill when opening (destroyOnHidden remounts the form each open, so this
41	  // re-seeds for whichever connection is being edited).
42	  useEffect(() => {
43	    if (open && connection) {
44	      form.setFieldsValue({ name: connection.name, config: connection.config });
45	    }
46	  }, [open, connection, form]);
47	
48	  const onFinish = async (values: FormValues) => {
49	    if (!connection) return;
50	    setSubmitting(true);
51	    try {
52	      await updateConnection(connection.id, {
53	        name: values.name,
54	        config: values.config ?? {},
55	      });
56	      message.success(`Connection “${values.name}” updated`);
57	      form.resetFields();
58	      onSaved();
59	    } catch (err) {
60	      message.error(`Update failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
61	    } finally {
62	      setSubmitting(false);
63	    }
64	  };
65	
66	  return (
67	    <Drawer
68	      title="Edit connection"
69	      open={open}
70	      onClose={onClose}
71	      width={520}
72	      destroyOnHidden
73	      footer={
74	        <Flex justify="end" gap={8}>
75	          <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
76	          <Button type="primary" loading={submitting} onClick={() => form.submit()}>
77	            Save
78	          </Button>
79	        </Flex>
80	      }
81	    >
82	      {connection && (
83	        <Form form={form} layout="vertical" onFinish={onFinish} requiredMark="optional">
84	          <Form.Item name="name" label="Name" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
85	            <Input />
86	          </Form.Item>
87	          {/* Type + env are fixed once a connection is created — show, don't edit. */}
88	          <Form.Item label="Type">
89	            <Typography.Text>{CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[connection.type]}</Typography.Text>
90	          </Form.Item>
91	          <Form.Item label="Environment">
92	            <Tag color={ENV_COLORS[connection.env]}>{envLabel(connection.env)}</Tag>
93	          </Form.Item>
94	          <ConnectionTypeFields type={connection.type} form={form} showSecret={false} />
95	        </Form>
96	      )}
97	    </Drawer>
98	  );
99	}
100
```

```
1	import { HistoryOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
2	import { App, Button, Drawer, Flex, Form, Input, Select, Typography } from 'antd';
3	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
4	
5	import type { ConnectionType } from '../../api/connections';
6	import { type Check, updateCheck } from '../../api/suites';
7	import { buildCheckPayload, configToForm } from './checkForm';
8	import { ConfigFieldItem, SeverityThresholdFields } from './checkFormFields';
9	import { CheckHistoryDrawer } from './CheckHistoryDrawer';
10	import { ColumnProfilePanel } from './ColumnProfilePanel';
11	import { DryRunPreview } from './DryRunPreview';
12	import { EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE, expectationsByCategoryFor } from './expectationCatalog';
13	
14	/**
15	 * Edit a GX check. The expectation Select (grouped by category) drives which
16	 * config fields render (from the expectation catalog); the submitted `config` is
17	 * rebuilt from only the selected expectation's declared fields, so switching
18	 * types never leaks stale kwargs. Creating a check is the dedicated
19	 * `/suites/:suiteId/checks/new` page, not this drawer.
20	 */
21	export function CheckDrawer({
22	  open,
23	  suiteId,
24	  check,
25	  target,
26	  connectionType,
27	  onClose,
28	  onSaved,
29	}: {
30	  open: boolean;
31	  suiteId: string;
32	  check?: Check;
33	  /** The suite's run target (#215) — drives the dry-run preview's table/schema. */
34	  target: Record<string, unknown> | null;
35	  /** The suite's datasource type — gates the Custom-SQL category (ADR 0019). */
36	  connectionType?: ConnectionType;
37	  onClose: () => void;
38	  onSaved: () => void;
39	}) {
40	  const { message } = App.useApp();
41	  const [form] = Form.useForm();
42	  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
43	  const [historyOpen, setHistoryOpen] = useState(false);
44	  const selectedType = Form.useWatch('expectation_type', form) as string | undefined;
45	  const column = Form.useWatch(['config', 'column'], form) as string | undefined;
46	  const spec = selectedType ? EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE[selectedType] : undefined;
47	
48	  // Reset first (antd keeps values for unmounted fields, so stale config can
49	  // survive an open→close→open with a different check), then prefill.
50	  useEffect(() => {
51	    if (!open || !check) return;
52	    form.resetFields();
53	    form.setFieldsValue({
54	      name: check.name,
55	      expectation_type: check.expectation_type,
56	      config: configToForm(EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE[check.expectation_type], check.config),
57	      warn_threshold: check.warn_threshold ?? undefined,
58	      fail_threshold: check.fail_threshold ?? undefined,
59	      critical_threshold: check.critical_threshold ?? undefined,
60	    });
61	  }, [open, check, form]);
62	
63	  // Close the history sub-drawer whenever this drawer closes or switches to a
64	  // different check — otherwise a left-open history would re-pop (showing the
65	  // new check) the next time the editor opens. Render-phase reset (the same
66	  // "adjust state when a prop changes" pattern as ImportSuiteDrawer's prevOpen),
67	  // not an effect, which can't setState synchronously.
68	  const histScope = open ? (check?.id ?? null) : null;
69	  const [histScopeSeen, setHistScopeSeen] = useState(histScope);
70	  if (histScope !== histScopeSeen) {
71	    setHistScopeSeen(histScope);
72	    setHistoryOpen(false);
73	  }
74	
75	  const onSubmit = async () => {
76	    if (!check) return;
77	    let values: Record<string, unknown>;
78	    try {
79	      values = await form.validateFields();
80	    } catch {
81	      return; // inline validation errors
82	    }
83	    const payload = buildCheckPayload(values);
84	    setSubmitting(true);
85	    try {
86	      await updateCheck(suiteId, check.id, payload);
87	      message.success(`${payload.name}: saved`);
88	      onSaved();
89	    } catch (err) {
90	      message.error(`Save failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);
91	    } finally {
92	      setSubmitting(false);
93	    }
94	  };
95	
96	  return (
97	    <Drawer
98	      title={check ? `Edit “${check.name}”` : 'Edit check'}
99	      open={open}
100	      onClose={onClose}
101	      width={520}
102	      destroyOnHidden
103	      extra={
104	        <Flex gap={8}>
105	          {check && (
106	            <Button icon={<HistoryOutlined />} onClick={() => setHistoryOpen(true)}>
107	              History
108	            </Button>
109	          )}
110	          <Button onClick={onClose}>Cancel</Button>
111	          <Button type="primary" loading={submitting} onClick={onSubmit}>
112	            Save
113	          </Button>
114	        </Flex>
115	      }
116	    >
117	      <Form form={form} layout="vertical">
118	        <Form.Item name="name" label="Name" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
119	          <Input placeholder="e.g. order_id not null" />
120	        </Form.Item>
121	        <Form.Item name="expectation_type" label="Expectation" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
122	          <Select
123	            placeholder="Select an expectation"
124	            // Grouped by category (antd optgroups) — the GX-Cloud-style picker.
125	            // Pass the check's current type so Custom SQL stays selectable when
126	            // editing one even before the connection type loads (else its
127	            // prefilled value would have no matching option).
128	            options={expectationsByCategoryFor(connectionType, check?.expectation_type).map(
129	              (g) => ({
130	                label: g.category,
131	                options: g.specs.map((e) => ({ value: e.type, label: e.label })),
132	              }),
133	            )}
134	          />
135	        </Form.Item>
136	
137	        {spec && (
138	          <>
139	            <Typography.Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ marginTop: -8 }}>
140	              {spec.description}
141	            </Typography.Paragraph>
142	            {spec.fields.map((field) => (
143	              <ConfigFieldItem key={field.name} field={field} />
144	            ))}
145	          </>
146	        )}
147	
148	        <SeverityThresholdFields />
149	
150	        <ColumnProfilePanel suiteId={suiteId} target={target} column={column} />
151	
152	        <DryRunPreview
153	          suiteId={suiteId}
154	          expectationType={selectedType}
155	          target={target}
156	          form={form}
157	        />
158	      </Form>
159	
160	      <CheckHistoryDrawer
161	        open={historyOpen}
162	        suiteId={suiteId}
163	        check={check ?? null}
164	        onClose={() => setHistoryOpen(false)}
165	      />
166	    </Drawer>
167	  );
168	}
169
```

```
1	import { api } from './client';
2	
3	/**
4	 * Suites API — a suite is a named bundle of DQ checks bound to one connection.
5	 * `connection_id` is set at create and immutable thereafter (re-pointing would
6	 * orphan the child checks), mirroring the backend `suite_service` contract.
7	 */
8	
9	/** Mirrors the backend `SuiteRead` schema. */
10	export interface Suite {
11	  id: string;
12	  name: string;
13	  description: string | null;
14	  connection_id: string;
15	  /** Datasource-shaped run target (#215); null = not yet runnable. */
16	  target: Record<string, unknown> | null;
17	  created_by: string;
18	  /** The caller's effective level — gates per-suite actions (share, delete).
19	   *  `owner`/`admin`/`edit`/`view`; absent on older payloads. */
20	  my_permission?: 'owner' | 'admin' | 'edit' | 'view' | null;
21	}
22	
23	/**
24	 * Owner/admin — may manage shares + delete the suite. The single source for the
25	 * "manage" gate so the suite-detail actions and any other surface never drift.
26	 */
27	export function canManageSuite(suite: Suite): boolean {
28	  return suite.my_permission === 'owner' || suite.my_permission === 'admin';
29	}
30	
31	/**
32	 * The `edit` capability ladder (owner/admin/edit) — may trigger/cancel runs and
33	 * manage triggers/schedules, mirroring the backend `POST /suites/{id}/run` gate.
34	 * Shared by the suite-detail Run button and the cross-suite Run-now panel so the
35	 * runnable-permission policy lives in one place.
36	 */
37	export function canRunSuite(suite: Suite): boolean {
38	  return canManageSuite(suite) || suite.my_permission === 'edit';
39	}
40	
41	/**
42	 * The datasource-shaped identity carried in `Suite.target` (#215): SQL targets
43	 * fill `table`/`schema`/`catalog`, flat-file targets fill `path`/`file_format`.
44	 * The wire shape is an untyped JSONB bag (`Record<string, unknown>`); read it
45	 * through `targetString` so the dry-run preview and column profiler don't each
46	 * re-hand-roll the `typeof x === 'string'` extraction.
47	 */
48	export interface RunTarget {
49	  table?: string;
50	  schema?: string;
51	  catalog?: string;
52	  path?: string;
53	  file_format?: 'csv' | 'parquet';
54	}
55	
56	/** Read one string field out of the untyped run-target bag, or `undefined`. */
57	export function targetString(
58	  target: Record<string, unknown> | null,
59	  key: keyof RunTarget,
60	): string | undefined {
61	  const value = target?.[key];
62	  return typeof value === 'string' ? value : undefined;
63	}
64	
65	/** Mirrors `SuiteCreate` — connection_id is required and immutable. `target` is
66	 *  optional (a suite may be created targetless = not-yet-runnable). */
67	export interface SuiteCreate {
68	  name: string;
69	  description?: string | null;
70	  connection_id: string;
71	  target?: RunTarget | null;
72	}
73	
74	/** Mirrors `SuiteUpdate` — name/description/target are mutable (connection isn't).
75	 *  A `null`/omitted `target` leaves the existing one unchanged (backend semantics:
76	 *  it never clears a target back to NULL). */
77	export interface SuiteUpdate {
78	  name?: string;
79	  description?: string | null;
80	  target?: RunTarget | null;
81	}
82	
83	export async function listSuites(params?: { connection_id?: string }): Promise<Suite[]> {
84	  const { data } = await api.get<Suite[]>('/suites', { params });
85	  return data;
86	}
87	
88	export async function getSuite(id: string): Promise<Suite> {
89	  const { data } = await api.get<Suite>(`/suites/${id}`);
90	  return data;
91	}
92	
93	export async function createSuite(payload: SuiteCreate): Promise<Suite> {
94	  const { data } = await api.post<Suite>('/suites', payload);
95	  return data;
96	}
97	
98	export async function updateSuite(id: string, payload: SuiteUpdate): Promise<Suite> {
99	  const { data } = await api.patch<Suite>(`/suites/${id}`, payload);
100	  return data;
101	}
102	
103	export async function deleteSuite(id: string): Promise<void> {
104	  await api.delete(`/suites/${id}`);
105	}
106	
107	/** Mirrors the backend `CheckRead` schema (read-only here — editor is a later slice). */
108	export interface Check {
109	  id: string;
110	  suite_id: string;
111	  name: string;
112	  kind: string;
113	  expectation_type: string;
114	  config: Record<string, unknown>;
115	  warn_threshold: number | null;
116	  fail_threshold: number | null;
117	  critical_threshold: number | null;
118	}
119	
120	export async function listChecks(suiteId: string): Promise<Check[]> {
121	  const { data } = await api.get<Check[]>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks`);
122	  return data;
123	}
124	
125	/** Mirrors `CheckCreate` — v1 only authors `kind: 'expectation'` (service-enforced). */
126	export interface CheckCreate {
127	  name: string;
128	  expectation_type: string;
129	  config: Record<string, unknown>;
130	  warn_threshold?: number | null;
131	  fail_threshold?: number | null;
132	  critical_threshold?: number | null;
133	}
134	
135	/** Mirrors `CheckUpdate` — all fields optional; kind is immutable. */
136	export interface CheckUpdate {
137	  name?: string;
138	  expectation_type?: string;
139	  config?: Record<string, unknown>;
140	  warn_threshold?: number | null;
141	  fail_threshold?: number | null;
142	  critical_threshold?: number | null;
143	}
144	
145	export async function createCheck(suiteId: string, payload: CheckCreate): Promise<Check> {
146	  const { data } = await api.post<Check>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks`, payload);
147	  return data;
148	}
149	
150	export async function updateCheck(
151	  suiteId: string,
152	  checkId: string,
153	  payload: CheckUpdate,
154	): Promise<Check> {
155	  const { data } = await api.patch<Check>(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}`, payload);
156	  return data;
157	}
158	
159	export async function deleteCheck(suiteId: string, checkId: string): Promise<void> {
160	  await api.delete(`/suites/${suiteId}/checks/${checkId}`);
161	}
162	
163	/**
164	 * Mirrors the backend `CheckVersionRead` — one immutable snapshot in a check's
```

```
1	import type { ConnectionType } from '../../api/connections';
2	import { type RunTarget, targetString } from '../../api/suites';
3	
4	/**
5	 * A suite's run target (#215) is datasource-shaped: SQL warehouses identify a
6	 * `table` (+ optional `schema`), Unity Catalog adds a required `catalog`, and
7	 * flat-file stores (ADLS / S3) identify a `path` (+ optional `file_format`).
8	 * `targetKind` collapses the six datasource types to the three input shapes the
9	 * editor renders; orchestration types never reach here (they can't back a suite).
10	 */
11	export type TargetKind = 'sql' | 'uc' | 'flatfile';
12	
13	export function targetKind(type: ConnectionType): TargetKind | null {
14	  switch (type) {
15	    case 'snowflake':
16	      return 'sql';
17	    case 'unity_catalog':
18	      return 'uc';
19	    case 'adls_gen2':
20	    case 's3':
21	      return 'flatfile';
22	    default:
23	      return null; // adf / airflow — not a datasource
24	  }
25	}
26	
27	/**
28	 * Collapse a stored run target to a one-line summary for read-only display:
29	 * flat files show their `path`; SQL / Unity Catalog show the dotted
30	 * `catalog.schema.table` (only the parts present). Returns `null` for a
31	 * targetless (not-yet-runnable) suite. Lives here next to the other
32	 * datasource-target-shape logic so a new target field has one owner.
33	 */
34	export function summarizeTarget(target: Record<string, unknown> | null): string | null {
35	  if (!target) return null;
36	  const path = targetString(target, 'path');
37	  if (path) return path;
38	  const parts = [
39	    targetString(target, 'catalog'),
40	    targetString(target, 'schema'),
41	    targetString(target, 'table'),
42	  ].filter((p): p is string => Boolean(p));
43	  return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join('.') : null;
44	}
45	
46	/** The raw target inputs the drawer collects (all optional strings). */
47	export interface TargetFormValues {
48	  target_table?: string;
49	  target_schema?: string;
50	  target_catalog?: string;
51	  target_path?: string;
52	  target_format?: 'csv' | 'parquet';
53	}
54	
55	/** Narrow an untyped stored `file_format` to the supported set, else `undefined`
56	 *  — the suite target is an untyped JSONB bag, so a stray value (e.g. `json`)
57	 *  must not prefill the Select with an option that doesn't exist. */
58	export function asFileFormat(value: string | undefined): 'csv' | 'parquet' | undefined {
59	  return value === 'csv' || value === 'parquet' ? value : undefined;
60	}
61	
62	export interface AssembledTarget {
63	  /** The target to send: `null` = leave targetless (no field was filled). */
64	  target: RunTarget | null;
65	  /** Set when the section was started but a required field is missing. */
66	  error?: { field: keyof TargetFormValues; message: string };
67	}
68	
69	const trimmed = (v?: string): string | undefined => {
70	  const t = v?.trim();
71	  return t ? t : undefined;
72	};
73	
74	/**
75	 * Turn the raw inputs into a `RunTarget` for the connection's datasource, mirroring
76	 * the backend `run_target.resolve_target` rules so a saved target is always
77	 * runnable. All-blank → `null` (a valid targetless suite). Partially filled but
78	 * missing the datasource's required field → an `error` naming that field, so the
79	 * UI flags it inline rather than letting the backend 422 on save.
80	 */
81	export function assembleTarget(kind: TargetKind, v: TargetFormValues): AssembledTarget {
82	  if (kind === 'flatfile') {
83	    const path = trimmed(v.target_path);
84	    if (!path && !v.target_format) return { target: null };
85	    if (!path) {
86	      return {
87	        target: null,
88	        error: { field: 'target_path', message: 'Path is required to run this suite.' },
89	      };
90	    }
91	    return { target: { path, ...(v.target_format ? { file_format: v.target_format } : {}) } };
92	  }
93	
94	  if (kind === 'sql') {
95	    const table = trimmed(v.target_table);
96	    const schema = trimmed(v.target_schema);
97	    if (!table && !schema) return { target: null };
98	    if (!table) {
99	      return {
100	        target: null,
101	        error: { field: 'target_table', message: 'Table is required to run this suite.' },
102	      };
103	    }
104	    return { target: { table, ...(schema ? { schema } : {}) } };
105	  }
106	
107	  // Unity Catalog: catalog + table required, schema optional.
108	  const catalog = trimmed(v.target_catalog);
109	  const table = trimmed(v.target_table);
110	  const schema = trimmed(v.target_schema);
111	  if (!catalog && !table && !schema) return { target: null };
112	  if (!catalog) {
113	    return {
114	      target: null,
115	      error: { field: 'target_catalog', message: 'Catalog is required to run this suite.' },
116	    };
117	  }
118	  if (!table) {
119	    return {
120	      target: null,
121	      error: { field: 'target_table', message: 'Table is required to run this suite.' },
122	    };
123	  }
124	  return { target: { catalog, table, ...(schema ? { schema } : {}) } };
125	}
126	
```
