{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/suites/SuiteForm.tsx",
  "content": "import { App, Button, Divider, Flex, Form, Input, Select, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n\nimport {\n  CONNECTION_KIND,\n  CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS,\n  type Connection,\n  envLabel,\n} from '../../api/connections';\nimport { createSuite, type Suite, targetString, updateSuite } from '../../api/suites';\nimport {\n  asFileFormat,\n  assembleTarget,\n  type TargetFormValues,\n  type TargetKind,\n  targetKind,\n} from './suiteTarget';\n\ninterface SuiteFormValues extends TargetFormValues {\n  name: string;\n  description?: string;\n  connection_id: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Create or edit a suite — the form body shared by the `/suites/new` page and the\n * `/suites/:id/edit` page (the drawer is retired in W6, ADR 0022). `suite ===\n * undefined` is create mode (connection is chosen then locked); editing exposes\n * name/description + the run target (`connection_id` is immutable on the backend —\n * re-pointing orphans child checks). The target is datasource-shaped (#215): the\n * fields shown depend on the selected connection's type, and the target is optional\n * (a suite may stay targetless = not-yet-runnable, which disables Run until set).\n */\nexport function SuiteForm({\n  suite,\n  connections,\n  onSaved,\n  onCancel,\n}: {\n  suite?: Suite;\n  /** Available connections for the create-mode picker. */\n  connections: Connection[];\n  /** Called with the saved suite (created or updated). */\n  onSaved: (suite: Suite) => void;\n  onCancel: () => void;\n}) {\n  const { message } = App.useApp();\n  const [form] = Form.useForm<SuiteFormValues>();\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const isEdit = suite !== undefined;\n  // A suite's connection is its datasource — orchestration providers (ADF/\n  // Airflow) are never queryable, so they can't back a suite (CLAUDE.md §4, #242).\n  const datasourceConnections = connections.filter((c) => CONNECTION_KIND[c.type] === 'datasource');\n\n  // The target fields follow the active connection's datasource type: fixed on\n  // edit, live-tracked from the picker on create.\n  const watchedConnId = Form.useWatch('connection_id', form);\n  const activeConnId = isEdit ? suite.connection_id : watchedConnId;\n  const activeConn = connections.find((c) => c.id === activeConnId);\n  const kind = activeConn ? targetKind(activeConn.type) : null;\n\n  // Prefill once on mount/edit; create starts blank.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (suite) {\n      form.setFieldsValue({\n        name: suite.name,\n        description: suite.description ?? undefined,\n        connection_id: suite.connection_id,\n        target_table: targetString(suite.target, 'table'),\n        target_schema: targetString(suite.target, 'schema'),\n        target_catalog: targetString(suite.target, 'catalog'),\n        target_path: targetString(suite.target, 'path'),\n        target_format: asFileFormat(targetString(suite.target, 'file_format')),\n      });\n    }\n  }, [suite, form]);\n\n  const onSubmit = async () => {\n    let values: SuiteFormValues;\n    try {\n      values = await form.validateFields();\n    } catch {\n      return; // validation errors render inline\n    }\n    // Assemble the datasource-shaped target; flag a partially-filled section\n    // inline rather than letting the backend 422 on save.\n    const { target, error } = kind ? assembleTarget(kind, values) : { target: null };\n    if (error) {\n      form.setFields([{ name: error.field, errors: [error.message] }]);\n      return;\n    }\n    // The backend update treats a null target as \"leave unchanged\" (it never\n    // clears a target back to NULL), so clearing the fields on a suite that has\n    // a target would silently keep the old one. Say so rather than no-op.\n    const hadTarget = isEdit && !!suite.target && Object.keys(suite.target).length > 0;\n    if (hadTarget && target === null) {\n      message.error('A run target can’t be removed once set — edit it to point elsewhere instead.');\n      return;\n    }\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const saved = isEdit\n        ? await updateSuite(suite.id, {\n            name: values.name,\n            description: values.description ?? null,\n            target,\n          })\n        : await createSuite({\n            name: values.name,\n            description: values.description ?? null,\n            connection_id: values.connection_id,\n            target,\n          });\n      message.success(`${values.name}: ${isEdit ? 'saved' : 'created'}`);\n      onSaved(saved);\n    } catch (err) {\n      message.error(`Save failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Form form={form} layout=\"vertical\" onFinish={onSubmit}>\n      <Form.Item name=\"name\" label=\"Name\" rules={[{ required: true }]}>\n        <Input placeholder=\"Daily Revenue Audit\" />\n      </Form.Item>\n      <Form.Item name=\"description\" label=\"Description (optional)\">\n        <Input.TextArea rows={3} placeholder=\"What this suite validates and why.\" />\n      </Form.Item>\n      <Form.Item\n        name=\"connection_id\"\n        label=\"Connection\"\n        rules={[{ required: true }]}\n        extra={isEdit ? 'The connection is fixed once a suite is created.' : undefined}\n      >\n        <Select\n          disabled={isEdit}\n          placeholder=\"Select a datasource connection\"\n          options={datasourceConnections.map((c) => ({\n            value: c.id,\n            label: `${c.name} · ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[c.type]} · ${envLabel(c.env)}`,\n          }))}\n        />\n      </Form.Item>\n      {kind && <TargetFields kind={kind} />}\n      <Flex justify=\"end\" gap={8}>\n        <Button onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</Button>\n        <Button type=\"primary\" htmlType=\"submit\" loading={submitting}>\n          {isEdit ? 'Save' : 'Create & add checks'}\n        </Button>\n      </Flex>\n    </Form>\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * The datasource-shaped run-target inputs. Optional as a whole (leave blank for a\n * not-yet-runnable suite); when started, the required field for the datasource is\n * enforced at submit by `assembleTarget`. Field names match `TargetFormValues`.\n */\nexport function TargetFields({ kind }: { kind: TargetKind }) {\n  return (\n    <>\n      <Divider style={{ marginTop: 4 }} />\n      <Flex vertical gap={2} style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>\n        <Typography.Text strong>Run target</Typography.Text>\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>\n          Where this suite’s checks run. Optional — required to run the suite.\n        </Typography.Text>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {kind === 'flatfile' ? (\n        <>\n          <Form.Item name=\"target_path\" label=\"File path\">\n            <Input placeholder=\"container/path/to/data.csv\" />\n          </Form.Item>\n          <Form.Item name=\"target_format\" label=\"File format\">\n            <Select\n              allowClear\n              placeholder=\"Infer from extension\"\n              options={[\n                { value: 'csv', label: 'CSV' },\n                { value: 'parquet', label: 'Parquet' },\n              ]}\n            />\n          </Form.Item>\n        </>\n      ) : (\n        <>\n          {kind === 'uc' && (\n            <Form.Item name=\"target_catalog\" label=\"Catalog\">\n              <Input placeholder=\"main\" />\n            </Form.Item>\n          )}\n          <Form.Item name=\"target_schema\" label=\"Schema (optional)\">\n            <Input placeholder={kind === 'uc' ? 'default' : 'PUBLIC'} />\n          </Form.Item>\n          <Form.Item name=\"target_table\" label=\"Table\">\n            <Input placeholder={kind === 'uc' ? 'orders' : 'ANALYTICS.ORDERS'} />\n          </Form.Item>\n        </>\n      )}\n    </>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/SuiteNew.tsx",
  "content": "import { Alert, Button, Card, Flex, Spin, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport { CONNECTION_KIND, listConnections } from '../api/connections';\nimport { SuiteForm } from '../components/suites/SuiteForm';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';\n\n/**\n * Dedicated full-page new-suite flow (ADR 0022 — replaces the create drawer):\n * name + description, datasource connection, and the run target. On create,\n * continues to the Add Check page so the suite is never left empty.\n */\nexport function SuiteNew() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(listConnections);\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12}>\n        <Flex vertical gap={2}>\n          <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n            New suite\n          </Typography.Title>\n          <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">\n            Define a validation suite, then add its checks.\n          </Typography.Text>\n        </Flex>\n        <Button onClick={() => navigate('/suites')}>Cancel</Button>\n      </Flex>\n\n      <Card size=\"small\">\n        <SuiteBody state={state} navigate={navigate} />\n      </Card>\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SuiteBody({\n  state,\n  navigate,\n}: {\n  state: ReturnType<typeof useAsyncData<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listConnections>>>>['state'];\n  navigate: ReturnType<typeof useNavigate>;\n}) {\n  if (state.status === 'loading') {\n    return <Spin tip=\"Loading connections…\" />;\n  }\n  if (state.status === 'error') {\n    return <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon title=\"Failed to load connections\" description={state.error} />;\n  }\n  const connections = state.data;\n  const hasDatasource = connections.some((c) => CONNECTION_KIND[c.type] === 'datasource');\n  if (!hasDatasource) {\n    return (\n      <Alert\n        type=\"info\"\n        showIcon\n        title=\"No datasource connections yet\"\n        description=\"A suite runs against a datasource. Add a Snowflake / flat-file / Unity Catalog connection first.\"\n      />\n    );\n  }\n  return (\n    <SuiteForm\n      connections={connections}\n      onCancel={() => navigate('/suites')}\n      // Continue to Add Check so a freshly-created suite is never left empty.\n      onSaved={(suite) => navigate(`/suites/${suite.id}/checks/new`)}\n    />\n  );\n}\n"
}

```
1	import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
2	
3	/** Three-state result of an async fetch. */
4	export type AsyncState<T> =
5	  | { status: 'loading' }
6	  | { status: 'ok'; data: T }
7	  | { status: 'error'; error: string };
8	
9	/**
10	 * Fetch on mount (and on `reload()`), with a cancelled-guard so a late
11	 * resolution after unmount doesn't set state, and rejection normalised to a
12	 * string `error`. Shared by the data pages (Home, Connections, …) so the
13	 * cancelled-effect dance lives in one place rather than being re-derived per page.
14	 *
15	 * `reload` re-runs the fetcher (e.g. after a mutation) while keeping the current
16	 * data visible until the refetch resolves — no flash back to the loading state.
17	 */
18	export function useAsyncData<T>(fetcher: () => Promise<T>): {
19	  state: AsyncState<T>;
20	  reload: () => void;
21	} {
22	  const [state, setState] = useState<AsyncState<T>>({ status: 'loading' });
23	  const [nonce, setNonce] = useState(0);
24	
25	  useEffect(() => {
26	    let cancelled = false;
27	    fetcher()
28	      .then((data) => {
29	        if (!cancelled) setState({ status: 'ok', data });
30	      })
31	      .catch((err: unknown) => {
32	        if (!cancelled) {
33	          setState({ status: 'error', error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
34	        }
35	      });
36	    return () => {
37	      cancelled = true;
38	    };
39	    // Re-run on mount and whenever `reload` bumps the nonce; the fetcher identity
40	    // is intentionally not a dependency.
41	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
42	  }, [nonce]);
43	
44	  const reload = useCallback(() => setNonce((n) => n + 1), []);
45	  return { state, reload };
46	}
47	
```

await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Table'), 'orders');\n\n    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Create & add checks/ }));\n\n    await waitFor(() => expect(mockCreateSuite).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));\n    expect(mockCreateSuite.mock.calls[0][0]).toMatchObject({\n      name: 'orders-suite',\n      connection_id: 'conn1',\n      target: { table: 1	import { Form, Input, Select, type FormInstance } from 'antd';
2	
3	import type { ConnectionType } from '../../api/connections';
4	import { CONNECTION_FORM_SPECS, type TextField } from './connectionFormSpec';
5	
6	/**
7	 * Renders the type-specific config + secret form fields from CONNECTION_FORM_SPECS.
8	 * Config fields are namespaced under `config` (name={['config','account']}) so the
9	 * drawer submits `config` as one object; the write-only credential is `secret`.
10	 */
11	
12	const requiredRule = [{ required: true }];
13	
14	function ConfigTextField({ field }: { field: TextField }) {
15	  return (
16	    <Form.Item
17	      name={['config', field.name]}
18	      label={field.optional ? `${field.label} (optional)` : field.label}
19	      rules={field.optional ? undefined : requiredRule}
20	    >
21	      <Input />
22	    </Form.Item>
23	  );
24	}
25	
26	function SecretField({ label, multiline = false }: { label: string; multiline?: boolean }) {
27	  return (
28	    <Form.Item name="secret" label={label} rules={requiredRule}>
29	      {multiline ? (
30	        <Input.TextArea rows={4} autoComplete="off" />
31	      ) : (
32	        <Input.Password autoComplete="off" />
33	      )}
34	    </Form.Item>
35	  );
36	}
37	
38	export function ConnectionTypeFields({
39	  type,
40	  form,
41	  showSecret = true,
42	}: {
43	  type: ConnectionType;
44	  form: FormInstance;
45	  /** Edit mode omits the secret — credential rotation is the Re-auth flow. */
46	  showSecret?: boolean;
47	}) {
48	  const spec = CONNECTION_FORM_SPECS[type];
49	  const authType = Form.useWatch(['config', 'auth_type'], form) as string | undefined;
50	  const activeAuth = spec.auth?.find((a) => a.value === authType) ?? spec.auth?.[0];
51	
52	  return (
53	    <>
54	      {spec.textFields.map((f) => (
55	        <ConfigTextField key={f.name} field={f} />
56	      ))}
57	
58	      {spec.auth && (
59	        <Form.Item name={['config', 'auth_type']} label="Auth type" rules={requiredRule}>
60	          <Select options={spec.auth.map((a) => ({ value: a.value, label: a.label }))} />
61	        </Form.Item>
62	      )}
63	
64	      {activeAuth?.extraField && <ConfigTextField field={activeAuth.extraField} />}
65	
66	      {showSecret &&
67	        (activeAuth ? (
68	          <SecretField label={activeAuth.secretLabel} multiline={activeAuth.multilineSecret} />
69	        ) : (
70	          spec.secretLabel && <SecretField label={spec.secretLabel} />
71	        ))}
72	    </>
73	  );
74	}
75

id: toolu_01VdHsw8Z9XPNryh4NaTgTuM
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/connections/ConnectionForm.tsx",
  "content": "import { App, Button, Flex, Form, Input, Select, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\n\nimport {\n  CONNECTION_ENVS,\n  CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS,\n  type Connection,\n  type ConnectionCreate,\n  type ConnectionType,\n  createConnection,\n  ENV_COLORS,\n  envLabel,\n  updateConnection,\n} from '../../api/connections';\nimport { ConnectionTypeFields } from './ConnectionTypeFields';\nimport { initialConfigForType } from './connectionFormSpec';\n\ninterface FormValues {\n  name: string;\n  env: ConnectionCreate['env'];\n  config?: Record<string, unknown>;\n  secret?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Create or edit a connection — the form body shared by the `/connections/new`\n * page (a type is picked first, then this renders) and the `/connections/:id/edit`\n * page (the drawer is retired in W6, ADR 0022). `connection === undefined` is\n * create mode (env is chosen + the credential is captured); editing locks type +\n * env (the backend `ConnectionUpdate` rejects changing them) and omits the secret\n * — credential rotation is the separate Re-auth flow.\n */\nexport function ConnectionForm({\n  type,\n  connection,\n  onSaved,\n  onCancel,\n}: {\n  /** The connection type — picked on the new page, fixed from the row on edit. */\n  type: ConnectionType;\n  connection?: Connection;\n  /** Called with the saved connection (created or updated). */\n  onSaved: (connection: Connection) => void;\n  onCancel: () => void;\n}) {\n  const { message } = App.useApp();\n  const [form] = Form.useForm<FormValues>();\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const isEdit = connection !== undefined;\n\n  // Seed the form: an edit prefills name + config; a create seeds the new type's\n  // config defaults (e.g. the auth-type) and clears any fields left over from a\n  // previously-picked type. Re-runs on `type`/`connection` so re-picking a type\n  // on the new page can't leak the prior type's fields.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    form.resetFields();\n    if (connection) {\n      form.setFieldsValue({ name: connection.name, config: connection.config });\n    } else {\n      form.setFieldsValue({ config: initialConfigForType(type) });\n    }\n  }, [type, connection, form]);\n\n  const onFinish = async (values: FormValues) => {\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const saved = isEdit\n        ? await updateConnection(connection.id, {\n            name: values.name,\n            config: values.config ?? {},\n          })\n        : await createConnection({\n            name: values.name,\n            type,\n            env: values.env,\n            config: values.config ?? {},\n            secret: values.secret || undefined,\n          });\n      message.success(`Connection “${values.name}” ${isEdit ? 'updated' : 'created'}`);\n      onSaved(saved);\n    } catch (err) {\n      message.error(\n        `${isEdit ? 'Update' : 'Create'} failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`,\n      );\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <Form form={form} layout=\"vertical\" onFinish={onFinish} requiredMark=\"optional\">\n      <Form.Item name=\"name\" label=\"Name\" rules={[{ required: true }]}>\n        <Input />\n      </Form.Item>\n      {isEdit ? (\n        // Type + env are fixed once a connection is created — show, don't edit.\n        <>\n          <Form.Item label=\"Type\">\n            <Typography.Text>{CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]}</Typography.Text>\n          </Form.Item>\n          <Form.Item label=\"Environment\">\n            <Tag color={ENV_COLORS[connection.env]}>{envLabel(connection.env)}</Tag>\n          </Form.Item>\n        </>\n      ) : (\n        <Form.Item name=\"env\" label=\"Environment\" rules={[{ required: true }]}>\n          <Select\n            options={CONNECTION_ENVS.map((e) => ({ value: e, label: envLabel(e) }))}\n            placeholder=\"Select an environment\"\n          />\n        </Form.Item>\n      )}\n      <ConnectionTypeFields type={type} form={form} showSecret={!isEdit} />\n      <Flex justify=\"end\" gap={8}>\n        <Button onClick={onCancel}>{isEdit ? 'Cancel' : 'Back'}</Button>\n        <Button type=\"primary\" htmlType=\"submit\" loading={submitting}>\n          {isEdit ? 'Save' : 'Create'}\n        </Button>\n      </Flex>\n    </Form>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

  "content": "import { Button, Card, Flex, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useState } from 'react';\nimport { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport {\n  CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS,\n  CONNECTION_KINDS,\n  CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS,\n  type ConnectionKind,\n  type ConnectionType,\n  typesOfKind,\n} from '../api/connections';\nimport { ConnectionForm } from '../components/connections/ConnectionForm';\n\n/**\n * Dedicated full-page add-connection flow (GX-Cloud style): pick a type from the\n * datasource / orchestration sections, then fill the type-specific form (shared\n * with the edit page via `ConnectionForm`). Editing an existing connection is the\n * dedicated `/connections/:id/edit` page.\n */\nexport function ConnectionNew() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [type, setType] = useState<ConnectionType>();\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12}>\n        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n          {type ? `New ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]} connection` : 'New connection'}\n        </Typography.Title>\n        <Button onClick={() => (type ? setType(undefined) : navigate('/connections'))}>\n          {type ? 'Back' : 'Cancel'}\n        </Button>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {type ? (\n        <Card size=\"small\">\n          <ConnectionForm\n            type={type}\n            onCancel={() => setType(undefined)}\n            onSaved={() => navigate('/connections')}\n          />\n        </Card>\n      ) : (\n        <Flex vertical gap={24}>\n          {CONNECTION_KINDS.map((kind) => (\n            <TypeSection key={kind} kind={kind} types={typesOfKind(kind)} onPick={setType} />\n          ))}\n        </Flex>\n      )}\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction TypeSection({\n  kind,\n  types,\n  onPick,\n}: {\n  kind: ConnectionKind;\n  types: ConnectionType[];\n  onPick: (type: ConnectionType) => void;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={12}>\n      <Typography.Title level={5} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n        {CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS[kind]}\n      </Typography.Title>\n      <Flex wrap gap={12}>\n        {types.map((type) => (\n          <Card\n            key={type}\n            hoverable\n            size=\"small\"\n            style={{ minWidth: 200 }}\n            onClick={() => onPick(type)}\n          >\n            <Typography.Text strong>{CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]}</Typography.Text>\n          </Card>\n        ))}\n      </Flex>\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/ConnectionEdit.tsx",
  "content": "import { Alert, Button, Card, Flex, Spin, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport { CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS, getConnection } from '../api/connections';\nimport { ConnectionForm } from '../components/connections/ConnectionForm';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';\n\n/**\n * Dedicated full-page edit-connection flow (ADR 0022 — replaces the edit drawer).\n * Type + env are immutable and shown read-only; the secret is omitted (rotation is\n * the separate Re-auth flow). Reuses `ConnectionForm` with the create page.\n */\nexport function ConnectionEdit() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { connectionId } = useParams<{ connectionId: string }>();\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => {\n    if (!connectionId) throw new Error('no connection');\n    return getConnection(connectionId);\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12}>\n        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n          {state.status === 'ok'\n            ? `Edit ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[state.data.type]} connection`\n            : 'Edit connection'}\n        </Typography.Title>\n        <Button onClick={() => navigate('/connections')}>Cancel</Button>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {state.status === 'loading' && <Spin tip=\"Loading connection…\" />}\n      {state.status === 'error' && (\n        <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon title=\"Failed to load connection\" description={state.error} />\n      )}\n      {state.status === 'ok' && (\n        <Card size=\"small\">\n          <ConnectionForm\n            type={state.data.type}\n            connection={state.data}\n            onCancel={() => navigate('/connections')}\n            onSaved={() => navigate('/connections')}\n          />\n        </Card>\n      )}\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/SuiteEdit.tsx",
  "content": "import { Alert, Button, Card, Flex, Spin, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport { listConnections } from '../api/connections';\nimport { getSuite } from '../api/suites';\nimport { SuiteForm } from '../components/suites/SuiteForm';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';\n\n/**\n * Dedicated full-page edit-suite flow (ADR 0022 — replaces the edit drawer).\n * Name + description + run target are editable; the connection is fixed (shown\n * read-only by `SuiteForm`). Reuses `SuiteForm` with the new-suite page.\n */\nexport function SuiteEdit() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { suiteId } = useParams<{ suiteId: string }>();\n  const back = () => navigate(suiteId ? `/suites/${suiteId}` : '/suites');\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(async () => {\n    if (!suiteId) throw new Error('no suite');\n    const [suite, connections] = await Promise.all([getSuite(suiteId), listConnections()]);\n    return { suite, connections };\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12}>\n        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n          {state.status === 'ok' ? `Edit “${state.data.suite.name}”` : 'Edit suite'}\n        </Typography.Title>\n        <Button onClick={back}>Cancel</Button>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {state.status === 'loading' && <Spin tip=\"Loading suite…\" />}\n      {state.status === 'error' && (\n        <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon title=\"Failed to load suite\" description={state.error} />\n      )}\n      {state.status === 'ok' && (\n        <Card size=\"small\">\n          <SuiteForm\n            suite={state.data.suite}\n            connections={state.data.connections}\n            onCancel={back}\n            onSaved={back}\n          />\n        </Card>\n      )}\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/pages/CheckEdit.tsx",
  "content": "import { HistoryOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';\nimport { Alert, App, Button, Card, Flex, Form, Input, Select, Spin, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport { getConnection } from '../api/connections';\nimport { type Check, getCheck, getSuite, updateCheck } from '../api/suites';\nimport { buildCheckPayload, configToForm } from '../components/checks/checkForm';\nimport { ConfigFieldItem, SeverityThresholdFields } from '../components/checks/checkFormFields';\nimport { CheckHistoryDrawer } from '../components/checks/CheckHistoryDrawer';\nimport { ColumnProfilePanel } from '../components/checks/ColumnProfilePanel';\nimport { DryRunPreview } from '../components/checks/DryRunPreview';\nimport { EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE, expectationsByCategoryFor } from '../components/checks/expectationCatalog';\nimport { useAsyncData } from '../hooks/useAsyncData';\n\n/**\n * Dedicated full-page edit-check flow (ADR 0022 — replaces the edit drawer). The\n * expectation Select (grouped by category) drives which config fields render; the\n * submitted `config` is rebuilt from only the selected expectation's declared\n * fields, so switching types never leaks stale kwargs. Creating a check is the\n * dedicated `/suites/:suiteId/checks/new` page. Version history is still a drawer\n * (the surviving read-only drawer alongside Share).\n */\nexport function CheckEdit() {\n  const { suiteId, checkId } = useParams<{ suiteId: string; checkId: string }>();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const back = () => navigate(suiteId ? `/suites/${suiteId}` : '/suites');\n  // Load the suite (target + datasource type) and the check together: the target\n  // drives the dry-run preview, the connection type gates Custom SQL (ADR 0019),\n  // and the check seeds the form.\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(async () => {\n    if (!suiteId || !checkId) throw new Error('no check');\n    const [suite, check] = await Promise.all([getSuite(suiteId), getCheck(suiteId, checkId)]);\n    // Best-effort: a suite may be readable while its connection isn't (shared\n    // suite). The connection only gates the Custom-SQL category.\n    const connection = await getConnection(suite.connection_id).catch(() => null);\n    return { suite, check, connection };\n  });\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12}>\n        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n          {state.status === 'ok' ? `Edit “${state.data.check.name}”` : 'Edit check'}\n        </Typography.Title>\n        <Button onClick={back}>Cancel</Button>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {state.status === 'loading' && <Spin tip=\"Loading check…\" />}\n      {state.status === 'error' && (\n        <Alert type=\"error\" showIcon title=\"Failed to load check\" description={state.error} />\n      )}\n      {state.status === 'ok' && suiteId && (\n        <Card size=\"small\">\n          <CheckEditForm\n            suiteId={suiteId}\n            check={state.data.check}\n            target={state.data.suite.target}\n            connectionType={state.data.connection?.type}\n            onCancel={back}\n            onSaved={back}\n          />\n        </Card>\n      )}\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction CheckEditForm({\n  suiteId,\n  check,\n  target,\n  connectionType,\n  onCancel,\n  onSaved,\n}: {\n  suiteId: string;\n  check: Check;\n  target: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n  connectionType?: import('../api/connections').ConnectionType;\n  onCancel: () => void;\n  onSaved: () => void;\n}) {\n  const { message } = App.useApp();\n  const [form] = Form.useForm();\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [historyOpen, setHistoryOpen] = useState(false);\n  const selectedType = Form.useWatch('expectation_type', form) as string | undefined;\n  const column = Form.useWatch(['config', 'column'], form) as string | undefined;\n  const spec = selectedType ? EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE[selectedType] : undefined;\n\n  // Seed from the loaded check once.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    form.setFieldsValue({\n      name: check.name,\n      expectation_type: check.expectation_type,\n      config: configToForm(EXPECTATION_BY_TYPE[check.expectation_type], check.config),\n      warn_threshold: check.warn_threshold ?? undefined,\n      fail_threshold: check.fail_threshold ?? undefined,\n      critical_threshold: check.critical_threshold ?? undefined,\n    });\n  }, [check, form]);\n\n  const onSubmit = async () => {\n    let values: Record<string, unknown>;\n    try {\n      values = await form.validateFields();\n    } catch {\n      return; // inline validation errors\n    }\n    setSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      await updateCheck(suiteId, check.id, buildCheckPayload(values));\n      message.success(`${values.name as string}: saved`);\n      onSaved();\n    } catch (err) {\n      message.error(`Save failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'unknown error'}`);\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <>\n      <Form form={form} layout=\"vertical\" onFinish={onSubmit}>\n        <Form.Item name=\"name\" label=\"Name\" rules={[{ required: true }]}>\n          <Input placeholder=\"e.g. order_id not null\" />\n        </Form.Item>\n        <Form.Item name=\"expectation_type\" label=\"Expectation\" rules={[{ required: true }]}>\n          <Select\n            placeholder=\"Select an expectation\"\n            // Grouped by category (antd optgroups). Pass the check's current type\n            // so Custom SQL stays selectable even before the connection loads.\n            options={expectationsByCategoryFor(connectionType, check.expectation_type).map((g) => ({\n              label: g.category,\n              options: g.specs.map((e) => ({ value: e.type, label: e.label })),\n            }))}\n          />\n        </Form.Item>\n\n        {spec && (\n          <>\n            <Typography.Paragraph type=\"secondary\" style={{ marginTop: -8 }}>\n              {spec.description}\n            </Typography.Paragraph>\n            {spec.fields.map((field) => (\n              <ConfigFieldItem key={field.name} field={field} />\n            ))}\n          </>\n        )}\n\n        <SeverityThresholdFields />\n\n        <Form.Item>\n          <ColumnProfilePanel suiteId={suiteId} target={target} column={column} />\n        </Form.Item>\n        <Form.Item>\n          <DryRunPreview\n            suiteId={suiteId}\n            expectationType={selectedType}\n            target={target}\n            form={form}\n          />\n        </Form.Item>\n\n        <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={8}>\n          <Button icon={<HistoryOutlined />} onClick={() => setHistoryOpen(true)}>\n            History\n          </Button>\n          <Flex gap={8}>\n            <Button onClick={onCancel}>Cancel</Button>\n            <Button type=\"primary\" htmlType=\"submit\" loading={submitting}>\n              Save\n            </Button>\n          </Flex>\n        </Flex>\n      </Form>\n\n      <CheckHistoryDrawer\n        open={historyOpen}\n        suiteId={suiteId}\n        check={check}\n        onClose={() => setHistoryOpen(false)}\n      />\n    </>\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

import { App as AntApp } from 'antd';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { type Connection, updateConnection } from '../../src/api/connections';
import { ConnectionDrawer } from '../../src/components/connections/ConnectionDrawer';

// The drawer is edit-only — creating a connection is the dedicated /connections/new
// page (ConnectionNew). So only the update path is mocked + exercised here.
vi.mock('../../src/api/connections', async (importOriginal) => {
  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/api/connections')>();
  return { ...actual, updateConnection: vi.fn() };
});

const mockUpdate = vi.mocked(updateConnection);

function renderDrawer(props: Partial<Parameters<typeof ConnectionDrawer>[0]> = {}) {
  return render(
    <AntApp>
      <ConnectionDrawer open onClose={vi.fn()} onSaved={vi.fn()} {...props} />
    </AntApp>,
  );
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.clearAllMocks();
});

describe('ConnectionDrawer — edit', () => {
  const existing: Connection = {
    id: 'c1',
    name: 'sf-dev',
    type: 'snowflake',
    env: 'dev',
    config: {
      account: 'acc1',
      user: 'svc',
      database: 'DB',
      schema: 'SC',
      warehouse: 'WH',
      auth_type: 'password',
    },
    has_secret: true,
    created_by: 'u1',
  };

  it('shows type + env read-only (immutable) with no way to change them', async () => {
    renderDrawer({ connection: existing });

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Account')).toHaveValue('acc1'));
    // Type + env are displayed, not editable — no Type/Environment Select.
    expect(screen.getByText('Snowflake')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('DEV')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Type')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Environment')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('prefills config, omits the secret field, and submits an update via PATCH', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onSaved = vi.fn();
    mockUpdate.mockResolvedValue({ ...existing, name: 'sf-dev-2' });
    renderDrawer({ connection: existing, onSaved });

    // Prefilled name + config (effect-driven, so wait for it).
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Account')).toHaveValue('acc1'));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Name')).toHaveValue('sf-dev');
    // Edit mode omits the secret — rotation is the Re-auth flow.
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Password')).not.toBeInTheDocument();

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Name'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'sf-dev-2');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      'c1',
      expect.objectContaining({
        name: 'sf-dev-2',
        config: expect.objectContaining({ account: 'acc1', auth_type: 'password' }),
      }),
    );
    expect(onSaved).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

describe('SuiteDrawer — edit', () => {
  const existing: Suite = {
    id: 's1',
    name: 'orders-suite',
    description: 'old desc',
    connection_id: 'conn1',
    target: null,
    created_by: 'u1',
  };

  it('prefills, locks the connection, and submits an update', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onSaved = vi.fn();
    mockUpdate.mockResolvedValue({ ...existing, name: 'orders-suite-2' });
    renderDrawer({ suite: existing, onSaved });

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Name')).toHaveValue('orders-suite'));
    // Connection is locked in edit mode.
    expect(screen.getByRole('combobox')).toBeDisabled();

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Name'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'orders-suite-2');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s1', {
      name: 'orders-suite-2',
      description: 'old desc',
      target: null,
    });
    expect(mockCreate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(onSaved).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('prefills the existing target and round-trips it on save', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockUpdate.mockResolvedValue(existing);
    renderDrawer({
      suite: { ...existing, target: { table: 'ANALYTICS.ORDERS', schema: 'PUBLIC' } },
    });

    // The target fields are prefilled from the suite's stored target.
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Table')).toHaveValue('ANALYTICS.ORDERS'));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Schema (optional)')).toHaveValue('PUBLIC');

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s1', {
      name: 'orders-suite',
      description: 'old desc',
      target: { table: 'ANALYTICS.ORDERS', schema: 'PUBLIC' },
    });
  });

  it('refuses to clear an existing target (backend keeps the last one)', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderDrawer({ suite: { ...existing, target: { table: 'ANALYTICS.ORDERS' } } });

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Table')).toHaveValue('ANALYTICS.ORDERS'));
    // Clearing the only target field and saving must not silently no-op.
    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Table'));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));

    expect(await screen.findByText(/can’t be removed once set/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mockUpdate).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

import { App as AntApp } from 'antd';
import { render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

import { type Check, listCheckVersions, updateCheck } from '../../src/api/suites';
import { CheckDrawer } from '../../src/components/checks/CheckDrawer';

// The drawer is edit-only — creating a check is the dedicated /checks/new page
// (CheckNew). So only the update path is mocked + exercised here.
vi.mock('../../src/api/suites', async (importOriginal) => {
  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('../../src/api/suites')>();
  return { ...actual, updateCheck: vi.fn(), listCheckVersions: vi.fn() };
});

const mockUpdate = vi.mocked(updateCheck);
const mockVersions = vi.mocked(listCheckVersions);

function renderDrawer(props: Partial<Parameters<typeof CheckDrawer>[0]> = {}) {
  return render(
    <AntApp>
      <CheckDrawer open suiteId="s1" target={null} onClose={vi.fn()} onSaved={vi.fn()} {...props} />
    </AntApp>,
  );
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.clearAllMocks();
});

describe('CheckDrawer — edit', () => {
  const existing: Check = {
    id: 'chk1',
    suite_id: 's1',
    name: 'amount range',
    kind: 'expectation',
    expectation_type: 'expect_column_values_to_be_between',
    config: { column: 'amount', min_value: 0, max_value: 100 },
    warn_threshold: 5,
    fail_threshold: 10,
    critical_threshold: null,
  };

  it('prefills config + thresholds and submits an update', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const onSaved = vi.fn();
    mockUpdate.mockResolvedValue(existing);
    renderDrawer({ check: existing, onSaved });

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Column')).toHaveValue('amount'));
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Warn ≥')).toHaveValue('5');

    await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Name'));
    await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'amount range v2');
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Save' }));

    await waitFor(() => expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(mockUpdate).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s1', 'chk1', {
      name: 'amount range v2',
      expectation_type: 'expect_column_values_to_be_between',
      config: { column: 'amount', min_value: 0, max_value: 100 },
      warn_threshold: 5,
      fail_threshold: 10,
      critical_threshold: null,
    });
    expect(onSaved).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('opens the version-history drawer from the History button (#280)', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockVersions.mockResolvedValue([
      {
        version_no: 1,
        name: 'amount range',
        kind: 'expectation',
        expectation_type: 'expect_column_values_to_be_between',
        config: { column: 'amount' },
        warn_threshold: null,
        fail_threshold: null,
        critical_threshold: null,
        changed_by: 'u1',
        changed_by_name: 'Ed Editor',
        created_at: '2026-06-15T10:00:00Z',
      },
    ]);
    renderDrawer({ check: existing });
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Column')).toHaveValue('amount'));

    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /History/ }));

    expect(await screen.findByText(/History — /)).toBeInTheDocument();
    await waitFor(() => expect(mockVersions).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s1', 'chk1'));
    expect(await screen.findByText('v1')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('closes the history sub-drawer when the edited check changes (#280)', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    mockVersions.mockResolvedValue([]);
    const { rerender } = renderDrawer({ check: existing });
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Column')).toHaveValue('amount'));
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /History/ }));
    expect(await screen.findByText(/History — /)).toBeInTheDocument();

    // Switch the editor to a different check — the left-open history must close,
    // not linger showing the previous check.
    const other: Check = { ...existing, id: 'chk2', name: 'row count' };
    rerender(
      <AntApp>
        <CheckDrawer
          open
          suiteId="s1"
          target={null}
          onClose={vi.fn()}
          onSaved={vi.fn()}
          check={other}
        />
      </AntApp>,
    );
    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.queryByText(/History — /)).not.toBeInTheDocument());
  });

  it('groups the expectation picker by category', async () => {
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    renderDrawer({ check: existing });

    await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Column')).toHaveValue('amount'));
    await user.click(screen.getByLabelText('Expectation'));
    // antd renders optgroup headers with the category labels.
    expect(
      await screen.findByText('Column values', { selector: '.ant-select-item-group' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByText('Table shape', { selector: '.ant-select-item-group' }),
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

async () => {\n    mockGetSuite.mockResolvedValue(suite);\n    mockGetCheck.mockResolvedValue(existing);\n    mockGetConnection.mockRejectedValue(new Error('forbidden'));\n    renderPage();\n\n    // 1	import {
2	  CloudOutlined,
3	  DeploymentUnitOutlined,
4	  FolderOpenOutlined,
5	  InboxOutlined,
6	  NodeIndexOutlined,
7	  TableOutlined,
8	} from '@ant-design/icons';
9	import { Flex } from 'antd';
10	import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
11	
12	import type { ConnectionType } from '../../api/connections';
13	
14	/**
15	 * Per-datasource visual identity (icon + brand-ish accent) so the connection
16	 * list reads as recognisable products rather than a wall of identical cards.
17	 * Single source for the glyph + colour — the card avatar and anywhere else that
18	 * wants a type marker both read from here.
19	 */
20	const TYPE_VISUAL: Record<ConnectionType, { icon: ReactNode; color: string }> = {
21	  snowflake: { icon: <CloudOutlined />, color: '#29b5e8' },
22	  adls_gen2: { icon: <FolderOpenOutlined />, color: '#0078d4' },
23	  s3: { icon: <InboxOutlined />, color: '#ff9900' },
24	  unity_catalog: { icon: <TableOutlined />, color: '#ff3621' },
25	  adf: { icon: <DeploymentUnitOutlined />, color: '#0078d4' },
26	  airflow: { icon: <NodeIndexOutlined />, color: '#017cee' },
27	};
28	
29	/** A rounded-square icon avatar tinted with the datasource's accent colour. */
30	export function ConnectionTypeAvatar({ type, size = 40 }: { type: ConnectionType; size?: number }) {
31	  const { icon, color } = TYPE_VISUAL[type];
32	  return (
33	    <Flex
34	      align="center"
35	      justify="center"
36	      style={{
37	        width: size,
38	        height: size,
39	        flexShrink: 0,
40	        borderRadius: 10,
41	        // A soft tint of the accent (≈12% alpha) behind the full-strength glyph.
42	        background: `${color}1f`,
43	        color,
44	        fontSize: size * 0.5,
45	      }}
46	    >
47	      {icon}
48	    </Flex>
49	  );
50	}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ/frontend/src/components/connections/connectionSources.ts",
  "content": "import { CONNECTION_TYPES, type ConnectionType } from '../../api/connections';\n\n/**\n * Presentation grouping for the add-connection source picker (ADR 0022 prototype).\n * Finer than the load-bearing datasource/orchestration split (`CONNECTION_KIND`):\n * it fans the four datasources into product-shaped buckets and — crucially — leads\n * with **Orchestration**, because DataQ's integration story starts there (watch the\n * provider's runs, then trigger suites on completion). Picker-only; the runtime\n * datasource-vs-orchestration distinction still flows through `CONNECTION_KIND`.\n */\nexport const SOURCE_CATEGORIES = [\n  'Orchestration',\n  'Warehouses',\n  'Lakehouses',\n  'Cloud Storage',\n] as const;\nexport type SourceCategory = (typeof SOURCE_CATEGORIES)[number];\n\nexport const SOURCE_CATEGORY: Record<ConnectionType, SourceCategory> = {\n  adf: 'Orchestration',\n  airflow: 'Orchestration',\n  snowflake: 'Warehouses',\n  unity_catalog: 'Lakehouses',\n  adls_gen2: 'Cloud Storage',\n  s3: 'Cloud Storage',\n};\n\n/** One-line \"what is this\" subtitle under each source's label in the picker. */\nexport const CONNECTION_BLURB: Record<ConnectionType, string> = {\n  snowflake: 'Cloud data warehouse',\n  unity_catalog: 'Databricks governance layer',\n  adls_gen2: 'Azure Data Lake Storage',\n  s3: 'Object storage buckets',\n  adf: 'Trigger & monitor pipeline runs',\n  airflow: 'Monitor DAG runs',\n};\n\n/** Lead-in copy shown under a category heading (only Orchestration has one). */\nexport const SOURCE_CATEGORY_NOTE: Partial<Record<SourceCategory, string>> = {\n  Orchestration:\n    'Add your orchestration provider first — DataQ integrates by watching its runs, then triggers suites on completion.',\n};\n\nexport interface SourceGroup {\n  category: SourceCategory;\n  types: ConnectionType[];\n  note?: string;\n}\n\n/** Source types grouped by category in display order (Orchestration first), each\n *  group in canonical `CONNECTION_TYPES` order. Empty categories are dropped. */\nexport function sourcesByCategory(): SourceGroup[] {\n  return SOURCE_CATEGORIES.map((category) => ({\n    category,\n    types: CONNECTION_TYPES.filter((t) => SOURCE_CATEGORY[t] === category),\n    note: SOURCE_CATEGORY_NOTE[category],\n  })).filter((g) => g.types.length > 0);\n}\n"
}

Editing an existing connection is the\n * dedicated `/connections/:id/edit` page.\n */\nexport function ConnectionNew() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [type, setType] = useState<ConnectionType>();\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: 640 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12}>\n        <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n          {type ? `New ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]} connection` : 'New connection'}\n        </Typography.Title>\n        <Button onClick={() => (type ? setType(undefined) : navigate('/connections'))}>\n          {type ? 'Back' : 'Cancel'}\n        </Button>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {type ? (\n        <Card size=\"small\">\n          <ConnectionForm\n            type={type}\n            onCancel={() => setType(undefined)}\n            onSaved={() => navigate('/connections')}\n          />\n        </Card>\n      ) : (\n        <Flex vertical gap={24}>\n          {CONNECTION_KINDS.map((kind) => (\n            <TypeSection key={kind} kind={kind} types={typesOfKind(kind)} onPick={setType} />\n          ))}\n        </Flex>\n      )}\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction TypeSection({\n  kind,\n  types,\n  onPick,\n}: {\n  kind: ConnectionKind;\n  types: ConnectionType[];\n  onPick: (type: ConnectionType) => void;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={12}>\n      <Typography.Title level={5} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n        {CONNECTION_KIND_LABELS[kind]}\n      </Typography.Title>\n      <Flex wrap gap={12}>\n        {types.map((type) => (\n          <Card\n            key={type}\n            hoverable\n            size=\"small\"\n            style={{ minWidth: 200 }}\n            onClick={() => onPick(type)}\n          >\n            <Typography.Text strong>{CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]}</Typography.Text>\n          </Card>\n        ))}\n      </Flex>\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}",
  "new_string": "import { RightOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';\nimport { Button, Card, Flex, Typography } from 'antd';\nimport { useState } from 'react';\nimport { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';\n\nimport { CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS, type ConnectionType } from '../api/connections';\nimport { ConnectionForm } from '../components/connections/ConnectionForm';\nimport {\n  CONNECTION_BLURB,\n  type SourceGroup,\n  sourcesByCategory,\n} from '../components/connections/connectionSources';\nimport { ConnectionTypeAvatar } from '../components/connections/connectionVisuals';\n\n/**\n * Dedicated full-page add-connection flow (GX-Cloud style): step 1 picks a source\n * from the categorized grid (Orchestration first — ADR 0022), step 2 fills the\n * type-specific form (shared with the edit page via `ConnectionForm`). Editing an\n * existing connection is the dedicated `/connections/:id/edit` page.\n */\nexport function ConnectionNew() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const [type, setType] = useState<ConnectionType>();\n\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={24} style={{ maxWidth: type ? 640 : 720 }}>\n      <Flex justify=\"space-between\" align=\"center\" gap={12}>\n        <Flex vertical gap={2}>\n          <Typography.Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0 }}>\n            {type ? `New ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]} connection` : 'New connection'}\n          </Typography.Title>\n          {!type && (\n            <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\">\n              Select a source or orchestration provider to connect.\n            </Typography.Text>\n          )}\n        </Flex>\n        <Button onClick={() => (type ? setType(undefined) : navigate('/connections'))}>\n          {type ? 'Back' : 'Cancel'}\n        </Button>\n      </Flex>\n\n      {type ? (\n        <Card size=\"small\">\n          <ConnectionForm\n            type={type}\n            onCancel={() => setType(undefined)}\n            onSaved={() => navigate('/connections')}\n          />\n        </Card>\n      ) : (\n        <Flex vertical gap={28}>\n          {sourcesByCategory().map((group) => (\n            <SourceSection key={group.category} group={group} onPick={setType} />\n          ))}\n        </Flex>\n      )}\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SourceSection({\n  group,\n  onPick,\n}: {\n  group: SourceGroup;\n  onPick: (type: ConnectionType) => void;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <Flex vertical gap={group.note ? 6 : 12}>\n      <Typography.Text\n        type=\"secondary\"\n        strong\n        style={{ fontSize: 12, letterSpacing: '0.05em', textTransform: 'uppercase' }}\n      >\n        {group.category}\n      </Typography.Text>\n      {group.note && (\n        <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ maxWidth: 560 }}>\n          {group.note}\n        </Typography.Text>\n      )}\n      <div\n        style={{\n          display: 'grid',\n          gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fill, minmax(300px, 1fr))',\n          gap: 14,\n        }}\n      >\n        {group.types.map((type) => (\n          <SourceCard key={type} type={type} onPick={onPick} />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </Flex>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction SourceCard({ type, onPick }: { type: ConnectionType; onPick: (t: ConnectionType) => void }) {\n  return (\n    <Card\n      hoverable\n      size=\"small\"\n      className=\"dq-card--interactive\"\n      onClick={() => onPick(type)}\n      aria-label={`Add ${CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]} connection`}\n    >\n      <Flex align=\"center\" gap={14}>\n        <ConnectionTypeAvatar type={type} size={44} />\n        <Flex vertical gap={2} style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>\n          <Typography.Text strong>{CONNECTION_TYPE_LABELS[type]}</Typography.Text>\n          <Typography.Text type=\"secondary\" style={{ fontSize: 13 }} ellipsis>\n            {CONNECTION_BLURB[type]}\n          </Typography.Text>\n        </Flex>\n        <RightOutlined style={{ color: '#bfbfbf' }} />\n      </Flex>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}"
}

Default htmlType is `'button'`, so the History and Cancel buttons won't submit the form. Good — no accidental-submit bug.

I have enough to finalize. Let me consolidate findings. The strongest concrete finding is the `useAsyncData` not refetching on route-param change across the three edit pages. Let me verify there's no `key` on the routes that would force remount, and confirm the failure path once more by checking if `Connections`/`Suites` list rows could ever navigate edit→edit. Actually, the most concrete reachable instance: a user on `/connections/A/edit` who uses browser **back then forward**, or has two connections and edits sequentially via... no. The cleanest reproducible case is deep-link + in-app `<Link>`-less. I'll report it scoped honestly.

Let me also double check: does `react-router` remount on param-only change? It does NOT by default. So stale data is real but only when the SAME edit route is navigated with a different param without an intermediate unmount. I'll include it as medium confidence.

Let me do one final scan of Angle A on `ConnectionForm` for the falsy-secret check: `secret: values.secret || undefined`. If a user legitimately entered a secret that's an empty string... empty string secret is meaningless, so `|| undefined` is correct. No falsy-zero issue (secrets aren't numeric).

Final answer:

Based on my three-angle review, here are the candidate bugs (highest-confidence). I verified and discarded several false leads (the antd `Alert title` prop is correct for antd v6; route ordering is fine under react-router v7 specificity ranking; the "can't remove target once set" guard and secret-omission-on-edit are correctly preserved; no stale drawer imports remain).

React Router reuses the same <ConnectionEdit/> element (no key, params-only change → no remount). useAsyncData's effect ([nonce] deps) does not re-run, so getConnection still holds A's data; the form stays prefilled with A and a Save would PATCH connection B with A's name/config. Same shape for SuiteEdit and CheckEdit (CheckEdit's seed effect is keyed on [check] which never updates, and the history drawer / dry-run reset all assume a fresh mount).",

Summary of what I checked and cleared (to save the caller time):
- Angle A: ConnectionForm's reset-on-`type`-change effect correctly clears prior-type fields; `secret: values.secret || undefined` correctly omits the secret on edit (no field rendered); History/Cancel buttons are antd default `htmlType="button"` so they don't accidentally submit; SuiteForm's "can't remove a target once set" guard is intact.
- Angle B: type/env immutability preserved (`ConnectionForm` shows them read-only via `isEdit`), secret omission preserved (`showSecret={!isEdit}`), suite `connection_id` locked on edit, expectation-config rebuild via `buildCheckPayload` unchanged. The `histScope` history-drawer reset and DryRunPreview in-place reset are not re-added but are naturally satisfied by per-route remount (only at risk under finding 1's no-remount path).
- Angle C: no stale `ConnectionDrawer`/`SuiteDrawer`/`CheckDrawer` imports remain; `getCheck`/`getSuite`/`getConnection` all exist and error-handle (`getConnection` is best-effort `.catch(() => null)`); navigate() targets all match registered routes; route registration is correct (react-router v7 ranks by specificity, so `/suites/new` vs `/suites/:suiteId` ordering is safe); `connectionSources` covers all 6 types via a total `Record<ConnectionType,...>` so no source is silently dropped.
- False positive avoided: the four new pages use `<Alert title=... />` — this is the CURRENT prop in antd v6 (`message` is the deprecated alias), so it is correct, not a bug.

The highest-value finding is #1 (shared root cause across all three edit pages); #2 is a downstream consequence of it.

> AGENT

Both passes are in. Consolidated verified findings:

1. **Correctness (real, low-reachability):** the three edit pages fetch via `useAsyncData`, whose effect keys on an internal nonce — not the route param. Navigating `/connections/A/edit` → `/connections/B/edit` param-only (no unmount) keeps A's data and would PATCH B with A's values. Same root cause drops CheckEdit's old `resetFields`-before-seed safety.
2. **Efficiency (minor):** CheckEdit awaits `getConnection` sequentially after the `Promise.all`.
3. **Altitude (cleanup):** the 3 edit pages duplicate the load+header+Card shell.

I'll fix the correctness issue (#1) with a keyed remount and the cheap fetch fix (#2). The shell duplication (#3) I'll leave as a noted follow-up rather than expand scope mid-phase. Let me apply the fixes:

  "old_string": "export function ConnectionEdit() {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { connectionId } = useParams<{ connectionId: string }>();\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => {\n    if (!connectionId) throw new Error('no connection');\n    return getConnection(connectionId);\n  });",
  "new_string": "export function ConnectionEdit() {\n  // Key the view by the id so a param-only navigation between two edit URLs\n  // (no unmount under react-router) remounts → refetches + reseeds, rather than\n  // leaving the previous connection's data in the form.\n  const { connectionId } = useParams<{ connectionId: string }>();\n  return <ConnectionEditView key={connectionId} connectionId={connectionId} />;\n}\n\nfunction ConnectionEditView({ connectionId }: { connectionId?: string }) {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(() => {\n    if (!connectionId) throw new Error('no connection');\n    return getConnection(connectionId);\n  });"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": " * dedicated `/suites/:suiteId/checks/new` page. Version history is still a drawer\n * (the surviving read-only drawer alongside Share).\n */\nexport function CheckEdit() {\n  const { suiteId, checkId } = useParams<{ suiteId: string; checkId: string }>();\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const back = () => navigate(suiteId ? `/suites/${suiteId}` : '/suites');\n  // Load the suite (target + datasource type) and the check together: the target\n  // drives the dry-run preview, the connection type gates Custom SQL (ADR 0019),\n  // and the check seeds the form.\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(async () => {\n    if (!suiteId || !checkId) throw new Error('no check');\n    const [suite, check] = await Promise.all([getSuite(suiteId), getCheck(suiteId, checkId)]);\n    // Best-effort: a suite may be readable while its connection isn't (shared\n    // suite). The connection only gates the Custom-SQL category.\n    const connection = await getConnection(suite.connection_id).catch(() => null);\n    return { suite, check, connection };\n  });",
  "new_string": " * dedicated `/suites/:suiteId/checks/new` page. Version history is still a drawer\n * (the surviving read-only drawer alongside Share). The fetch + form live in a\n * view keyed on the check id so a param-only route change reloads cleanly.\n */\nexport function CheckEdit() {\n  const { suiteId, checkId } = useParams<{ suiteId: string; checkId: string }>();\n  return <CheckEditView key={checkId} suiteId={suiteId} checkId={checkId} />;\n}\n\nfunction CheckEditView({ suiteId, checkId }: { suiteId?: string; checkId?: string }) {\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\n  const back = () => navigate(suiteId ? `/suites/${suiteId}` : '/suites');\n  // Load the suite (target + datasource type) and the check together: the target\n  // drives the dry-run preview, the connection type gates Custom SQL (ADR 0019),\n  // and the check seeds the form. The connection only depends on the suite, so it\n  // chains off getSuite while getCheck runs alongside (not serially after both).\n  const { state } = useAsyncData(async () => {\n    if (!suiteId || !checkId) throw new Error('no check');\n    const suiteP = getSuite(suiteId);\n    // Best-effort: a suite may be readable while its connection isn't (shared\n    // suite). The connection only gates the Custom-SQL category.\n    const connectionP = suiteP.then((s) => getConnection(s.connection_id)).catch(() => null);\n    const [suite, check, connection] = await Promise.all([\n      suiteP,\n      getCheck(suiteId, checkId),\n      connectionP,\n    ]);\n    return { suite, check, connection };\n  });"
}

178:- [x] ✅ Add connection drawer — type-specific form fields per connection type — spec-driven `ConnectionTypeFields` from `CONNECTION_FORM_SPECS` (all six types; Snowflake password/key-pair auth toggle) — [PR #196](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/196) (+ Snowflake key-pair backend [PR #193](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/193))
180:- [x] ✅ Connection re-auth UI — surface expired tokens, inline refresh action — `ReauthModal` (single-secret rotate+verify) + edit drawer + delete via the card actions menu — [PR #198](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/198)
181:- [x] ✅ ADLS/S3 connection form — account URL, SAS toggle — covered by the spec-driven drawer ([PR #196](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/196)) _(container browser + managed-identity/IAM-role modes deferred with the backend, ADR 0010/0011)_
182:- [x] ✅ Databricks connection form — workspace URL, PAT, warehouse id — covered by the spec-driven drawer ([PR #196](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/196)) _(live SQL-Warehouse picker deferred; warehouse id is a text field)_
187:- [x] ✅ Flat file check editor — file path + format selector — the datasource-shaped **run-target editor** on the suite drawer ([PR #246](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/246)) authors a flat-file target (`path` + optional CSV/Parquet `file_format`), so ADLS/S3 suites become runnable; the generic catalog editor ([PR #203](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/203)) already covers flat-file expectations. _(Container/regex **batch-resolution** inputs remain a post-v1 polish — needs the flat-file listing surface.)_
189:- [x] ✅ **Monaco SQL editor for custom check** — a "Custom SQL" category authoring a GX `UnexpectedRowsExpectation` (ADR 0019): a lazy-loaded, locally-bundled Monaco editor (`SqlEditorField`, its own `monaco` Vite chunk so the eager bundle is untouched) + a client-side mirror of the backend read-only/single-statement guardrail (`customSql.ts`) for inline feedback. Gated to SQL datasources (Snowflake / Unity Catalog) in both the create page and the edit drawer via `expectationsByCategoryFor(connectionType)`. _(backend unblocked by ADR 0019 / [PR #258](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/258); Snowflake keyword autocomplete deferred — Monaco ships SQL syntax highlighting only)_
190:- [x] ✅ Column profiler panel — inline in check editor, loads on table / file selection — `ColumnProfilePanel` (shared by the create page `CheckNew` + the edit drawer `CheckDrawer`): a collapsed-by-default panel that profiles one column of the suite's run target (#215) via `POST /suites/{id}/profile` (no persistence) and renders null count/fraction, distinct count, min/max, and top values — so the author can ground a range / allowed-set / null threshold in real data before saving. The profiled column **pre-fills from the check's `column` config field** (render-phase prop-sync, like `DryRunPreview`) but stays editable to explore neighbouring columns; `table`/`schema`/`catalog` (SQL) or `path`/`file_format` (flat file) are pulled from the suite target. Disabled with a reason until the suite has a table/file target + a column is entered; backend limits (no credential / unreachable / unsupported type) surface as a clean error alert. 4 vitest (disabled-states / pre-filled-profile with request-shape assertion / error). _(PR-D2; `ColumnProfilePanel.tsx` + `api/suites.ts` `profileColumns`)_
191:- [x] ✅ **Check dry-run button** — preview pass/fail inline before saving — `DryRunPreview` component (shared by the create page `CheckNew` + the edit drawer `CheckDrawer`): runs the in-progress check against the suite's live target via `POST /suites/{id}/checks/dryrun` and renders the severity outcome (status tag + `metric_value` + observed/expected), **no persistence**. Reuses `buildCheckPayload` (so the preview runs exactly the saved check's config/thresholds) + the severity colour map; `table`/`schema` come from the suite's run target (#215, fetched via new `getSuite`). Disabled with a reason until an expectation is picked + the suite has a table target; backend limits (Snowflake-only, no-credential/unreachable) surface as a clean error alert (dev fail-softs to a 502 with fake creds). 4 vitest (disabled-states / success-outcome with request-shape assertion / error) + an e2e button-enabled assertion. _(PR-D1; `DryRunPreview.tsx` + `api/suites.ts` `getSuite`/`dryRunCheck`)_
192:- [x] ✅ **Check version history drawer — see previous config before overwriting** — a `check_versions` table snapshots a check's editable state (name/expectation/config/thresholds + author) on create, on every *real* update (no-op PATCHes skipped via `session.is_modified`), and per imported check; `GET /suites/{id}/checks/{cid}/versions` (view-gated, newest-first, author resolved server-side). The **History** button on `CheckDrawer` opens `CheckHistoryDrawer` — newest-first list, latest tagged **Current**, each row showing expectation label + pretty-printed config + `Warn ≥/Fail ≥/Critical ≥` thresholds + author/timestamp. View-only; **restore deferred to [#283](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/283)**. Backend migration verified up/down; 7 backend + 6 frontend tests. _(#280; backend [PR #281](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/281) + frontend [PR #282](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/pull/282); ADR 0019 custom-SQL configs stay readable via pretty-print)_
196:- [x] ✅ Suite sharing panel — add / remove users, assign roles inline — **Share** button on the suite detail opens `SharePanel` (drawer): lists collaborators by name (from the enriched `ShareRead`) and, for owner/admin, adds/changes/removes them. Add picker is a debounced `GET /users/search` type-ahead filtering out the owner + already-shared users; permission is the view/edit/admin ladder. Gated on **`SuiteRead.my_permission`** — a new field stamping the caller's effective level (owner/admin/edit/view), populated on create/get/update + batched across the list via `suite_authz.effective_permissions` (one shares `IN`-query, no N+1) — so a non-owner admin gets the manage controls and a viewer a read-only list. Backend enablers landed first: **PR-E2** (`GET /users/search` behind a FastAPI-free `user_service` — case-insensitive email/display_name substring, 2-char min, limit≤50, LIKE-escaped — + `ShareRead` enriched with `email`/`display_name` via a joined read-only `Share.user`). 6 component tests (list-by-name, empty, search→grant with owner/existing filtered, change, revoke, read-only non-admin) + backend `my_permission` + `/users/search` tests. _(PR-E2 + PR-E3)_
198:- [x] ✅ Suite export / import UI (download JSON, upload JSON) — **Export** button on the suite detail header downloads `<suite>.json` from `GET /suites/{id}/export` via a transient-anchor blob (`utils/download.ts` — `downloadJson` + `toFilenameStem`); **Import** button on the suites header opens `ImportSuiteDrawer` — drag/drop a `.json`, client-side parse + shape-check (fail-fast on non-suite/malformed JSON), pick a target connection, `POST /suites/import`. The parsed document is handed back **untouched** so thresholds round-trip exactly (string-or-number Decimal encoding never coerced). 11 tests (download lib: stem slug + blob/anchor/revoke; drawer: valid→import-with-faithful-document, non-suite/malformed-JSON rejection, connection-gated Import). _(PR-E1; backend ready from Week 3)_

Decided: in-app results page (Grafana deferred to an optional post-v1 ops add-on, never the per-suite authz path — ADR 0018 pending); full-page create + drawer edit; connections/checks redesign now (backends ready), results after the Week-5 run-enablement backend.

207:**Week 4 total: 26 / 26 ✅ — complete** _(connection manager UI complete (6/6); check editor core shipped + **dry-run preview** (PR-D1) + **column profiler panel** (PR-D2) + the datasource-shaped **run-target editor** (PR #246 — closes the flat-file + Unity Catalog target inputs, making suites runnable); **check version-history drawer** (#280; backend #281 + frontend #282 — view-only, restore deferred to #283); **suite export/import UI** (PR-E1); **suite sharing panel** (PR-E2 backend + PR-E3 UI, gated on `SuiteRead.my_permission`); **GX-Cloud redesign complete (4/4)** — classified connection + check create pages, edit-only drawers, suite-route/categorized picker (PR-A1/A2/B1/B2); PR-3c polish 3/3 (Settings `extra="forbid"` shipped via the `.env`/`.env.app` split, #209); **Monaco custom-SQL editor** (ADR 0019 — custom-SQL as a GX `UnexpectedRowsExpectation`; backend #258 + frontend #259); **admin control centre** (#289 — workspace-admin authz + `/admin/{suites,users,access}` + the `/admin` page & 403, pulling the Week-7 prototype-adoption admin tasks forward))_

Why: the [DataQ Design System](#week-6--results-dashboard--alerting) prototype makes the **Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard** (`/dashboard`, the [Week-6 task](#week-6--results-dashboard--alerting)) the page-home for the recent-runs widget, and its row drill-down target is the Week-6 routed run-detail page (`/results/:runId`, [#268](https://github.com/TheurgicDuke771/DataQ/issues/268) — promotes `Results.tsx`'s in-page `openRun` state to a route). Building a standalone table on `/results` now and rebuilding it on `/dashboard` in W6 is throwaway work; the dashboard also gates on the chart-library decision first. Deferred to build it **once, in its final home** — see Week 6.
239:- [x] ✅ Run now panel — suite picker, env / datasource, notification target — `RunNowPanel` (`components/runs/`): a **cross-suite** run launcher (modal off a **Run now** button in the Results header), distinct from the suite-detail **Run** button (#245) which runs one suite in context. A searchable suite `Select` filtered to the `owner`/`admin`/`edit` ladder the backend `POST /suites/{id}/run` enforces (view-only suites never appear); a read-only `Descriptions` readout of the picked suite's **env** (tag) + **datasource** (connection name + type) + **target** (one-line `catalog.schema.table` / flat-file `path`, mirroring `suiteTarget.ts`). A targetless suite blocks **Run** with an inline reason rather than a backend 422. On trigger it hands off to the shared `LiveRunProgress` drawer (modal closes; run watched + cancellable). **Notification target ships as a clearly-labelled disabled placeholder** ("coming in Week 6") — no notification backend exists yet (Teams/`ResultPublisher` alert routing is Week 6), so it doesn't jump the gun. Self-contained (owns its data fetch + progress drawer) so the Week-6 UI rework can drop it onto the dedicated Execution page unchanged. 3 component tests (no-runnable empty state, env/datasource/target render + trigger, targetless-suite block).
240:- [x] ✅ Live run progress UI — check-by-check status with spinner + cancel button — `LiveRunProgress` drawer (`components/runs/`): opens on the run id returned by the manual **Run** button (#245) instead of bouncing to `/results`, and polls `GET /runs/{id}/progress` (#301) on a self-scheduling timeout (not `setInterval` — no overlapping fetches) until the run is terminal (`succeeded`/`failed`/`cancelled`), then stops. Shows the run-status tag + a completed/total `Progress` bar + a per-check list (a `Spin`+"pending" while a check's `status` is null, its severity `Tag` once resolved). An editor (`canRun`) gets a **Cancel** button (`POST /runs/{id}/cancel` #302) on a non-terminal run; cancel reflects the terminal state immediately + the next poll confirms it. On terminal, a "View full results →" link to `/results`. Body keyed by run id so opening a new run restarts polling; `pollMs` is injectable for deterministic tests. 7 component tests (pending/resolved render, poll-until-terminal-then-stop, cancel happy path, cancel hidden when terminal / non-editor, fetch-error alert). **(A3)**
242:- [ ] 🔵 Recent runs audit table with drill-down link to results — **moved to Week 6** (counted there), folded into the Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard (`/dashboard`) where the prototype homes the recent-runs widget. Rationale in the group note above + the Week-6 task.

249:## Week 6 — Results dashboard + alerting
255:- [x] ✅ **Results page scaffold** (PR-C1) — the in-app `/results` page + sidebar nav (Connections · Suites · **Results** · Profile). **Runs tab**: a runs table (suite name, status, triggered-by, started, duration) with a status filter, each row opening a **run-detail drawer** that drills into the per-check results (check name + expectation, severity tag, `metric_value`, observed value). **Pipeline runs tab**: the orchestration monitoring feed (`pipeline_runs`) with a provider filter. Pure presentation helpers (`resultsFormat.ts` — duration/timestamp formatters, status→colour maps) are unit-tested; `api/runs.ts` mirrors the C0b schemas. Demo seed (`demo_data.py`) now lands runs + results (pass/pass/warn/fail spread) + two pipeline-runs + suite targets so the page has real content. 10 vitest + 2 Playwright (`results.spec.ts`). _The rich widgets below build on this scaffold._
277:> Adopt the **DataQ Design System** prototype (claude.ai/design project *"DataQ Design System"*, reverse-engineered from the shipped indigo/antd app — its `readme.md` is the spec) — **the full 13-screen set this week**, not a subset (ADR [0022](adr/0022-week6-prototype-adoption-and-chart-library.md); the earlier 6-task scope under-counted it — Profile content, **Settings** (pulled in from Week 7), the Admin layout-reconcile, and the New-Connection source-select split were missing). The **foundation already matches** the product (`theme.ts` palette, `resultsFormat.ts` status model, the 56px-header/220px-sider shell), so this is **not a re-skin** — it adopts the prototype's **structure**: every create/edit flow becomes a dedicated, deep-linkable **page** (the **Share** drawer stays the *only* drawer), login lands on an **Enhanced Monitoring Dashboard**, and standard **error pages** ship. **Where the prototype differs from the shipped app (drawer-vs-page, layout), the prototype wins.** Components are reproduced with **real antd** — the prototype's framework-free recreations + `_ds_bundle.js` are reference only (per the design README). Post-v1 items (marketing, dark mode) + the polish passes (KPI honesty, visual-fidelity) stay in Week 7 / Post-v1 below. The intentionally-dropped prototype bits (the **"View as" role switch**, the **component bundle**, the **teal palette**) are recorded in Post-v1 as *not adopted*.

> AGENT

Phase 4 is complete. Here's the summary.

## Week 6 Phase 4 — Drawer → page restructures ✅

Branch `feature/w6-phase4-drawer-to-page`, 5 commits, all gates green locally (typecheck · lint · prettier · **259 tests** · production build).

**4.1 — New Suite page** ([1f437fa](#)) — extracted a shared `SuiteForm` (name/description/connection picker + run-target inputs) out of `SuiteDrawer`; [`/suites/new`](frontend/src/pages/SuiteNew.tsx) reuses it and, on create, continues to `/suites/:id/checks/new` so a suite is never left empty.

**4.2 — Edit flows → pages** ([95c7cd2](#)) — `/connections/:id/edit`, `/suites/:id/edit`, `/suites/:id/checks/:checkId/edit` as deep-linkable pages reusing the create forms (`ConnectionForm`/`SuiteForm` gained an edit mode). **Removed `ConnectionDrawer`, `SuiteDrawer`, `CheckDrawer`** + their tests; parents navigate instead of holding drawer state. Added `getCheck`. Version history stays the one read-only drawer alongside Share. Dedups #204.

**4.3 — Connection source-select split** ([94af936](#)) — categorized picker (Orchestration first → Warehouses/Lakehouses/Cloud Storage) with avatar + blurb + chevron cards; grouping is presentation-only in [connectionSources.ts](frontend/src/components/connections/connectionSources.ts), the load-bearing split still flows through `CONNECTION_KIND`.

**Review** — ran the multi-angle `/code-review`. It surfaced one real correctness bug: the edit pages fetched via `useAsyncData` keyed on an internal nonce, so a param-only navigation between two edit URLs would keep stale data and could PATCH the wrong id. Fixed ([ddbdc1d](#)) by keying each edit view on its route id (forces remount → refetch + reseed, with a regression test) and parallelized CheckEdit's `getConnection`. One deferred cleanup noted (3 edit pages share a load+header+Card shell → an `EditPageShell` wrapper, natural to fold in when Phase 6 adds the 404 branches).

Docs: `progress.md` + `WEEK6_TODO.md` ticked (Week 6 now **16/27**); epic #175 mirror synced.

Nothing is pushed — the branch is local. Want me to open the PR, or move on to Phase 5 (Profile / Settings / Admin reconcile)?
